```
  4640: void CItemManager::GCItemListSend(int aIndex) // OK

  4644: 	BYTE send[4096];

  4646: 	PMSG_ITEM_LIST_SEND pMsg;
  4647: 
  4648: 	pMsg.header.set(0xF3,0x10,0);
  4649: 
  4650: 	int size = sizeof(pMsg);
  4651: 
  4652: 	pMsg.count = 0;
  4653: 
  4654: 	PMSG_ITEM_LIST info;

  4656: 	for(int n=0;n < INVENTORY_SIZE;n++)
  4657: 	{

  4661: 			{
  4662: 				info.slot = n;
  4663: 
  4664: 				this->ItemByteConvert(info.ItemInfo,lpObj->Inventory[n]);
  4665:

  4668: 
  4669: 				pMsg.count++;
  4670: 			}
  4671: 		}
  4672: 	}
  4673: 
  4674: 	pMsg.header.size[0] = SET_NUMBERHB(size);
  4675: 	pMsg.header.size[1] = SET_NUMBERLB(size);
  4676: 
  4677: 	memcpy(send,&pMsg,sizeof(pMsg));
  4678: 
  4679: 	DataSend(aIndex,send,size);
  4680: }
```

```
   115: void CShop::ShopItemSet(int slot,BYTE type) // OK
   116: {
   117: 	if(SHOP_INVENTORY_RANGE(slot) == 0)
   118: 	{
   119: 		return;
   120: 	}
   121: 
   122: 	ITEM_INFO ItemInfo;
   123: 
   124: 	if(gItemManager.GetInfo(this->m_Item[slot].m_Index,&ItemInfo) == 0)
   125: 	{
   126: 		return;
   127: 	}
   128: 
   129: 	int x = slot%8;
   130: 	int y = slot/8;
   131: 
   132: 	if((x+ItemInfo.Width) > 8 || (y+ItemInfo.Height) > 15)
   133: 	{
   134: 		return;
   135: 	}
   136: 
   137: 	for(int sy=0;sy < ItemInfo.Height;sy++)
   138: 	{
   139: 		for(int sx=0;sx < ItemInfo.Width;sx++)
   140: 		{
   141: 			this->m_InventoryMap[(((sy+y)*8)+(sx+x))] = type;
   142: 		}
   143: 	}
   144: }
   145: 
   146: BYTE CShop::ShopRectCheck(int x,int y,int width,int height) // OK
   147: {
   148: 	if((x+width) > 8 || (y+height) > 15)
   149: 	{
   150: 		return 0xFF;
   151: 	}
   152: 
   153: 	for(int sy=0;sy < height;sy++)
   154: 	{
   155: 		for(int sx=0;sx < width;sx++)

   157: 			if(this->m_InventoryMap[(((sy+y)*8)+(sx+x))] != 0xFF)
   158: 			{
   159: 				return 0xFF;
   160: 			}
   161: 		}
   162: 	}
   163: 
   164: 	return ((y*8)+x);
```

```
   297: bool CShop::GCShopItemListSend(int aIndex) // OK
   298: {
   299: 	BYTE send[2048];
   300: 
   301: 	PMSG_SHOP_ITEM_LIST_SEND pMsg;
   302: 
   303: 	pMsg.header.set(0x31,0);
   304: 
   305: 	int size = sizeof(pMsg);
   306: 
   307: 	pMsg.type = 0;
   308: 
   309: 	pMsg.count = 0;
   310: 
   311: 	PMSG_SHOP_ITEM_LIST info;
   312: 
   313: 	for(int n=0;n < SHOP_SIZE;n++)
   314: 	{
   315: 		if(this->m_Item[n].IsItem() != 0)
   316: 		{
   317: 			info.slot = n;
   318: 
   319: 			gItemManager.ItemByteConvert(info.ItemInfo,this->m_Item[n]);
   320: 
   321: 			memcpy(&send[size],&info,sizeof(info));
   322: 			size += sizeof(info);
   323: 
   324: 			pMsg.count++;
   325: 		}
   326: 	}
   327: 
   328: 	pMsg.header.size[0] = SET_NUMBERHB(size);
   329: 	pMsg.header.size[1] = SET_NUMBERLB(size);
   330: 
   331: 	memcpy(send,&pMsg,sizeof(pMsg));
   332: 
   333: 	DataSend(aIndex,send,size);
   334: 
   335: 	this->GCItemValueSend(aIndex);
   336: 	return 1;
   337: }
```

```
   122: 			require[2] = gItemManager.GetInventoryItemCount(lpObj,GET_ITEM(12,141),1);

   124: 			break;
   125: 	}

   127: 	(*count) = require[0]+(require[1]*10)+(require[2]*20)+(require[3]*30);

   133: 		value -= require[3]*30;
   134: 	}
   135: 
   136: 	if(require[2] > 0)
   137: 	{

   141: 	}
   142:

   161: 		table[2] = require[2];
   162: 		table[3] = require[3];

   166: void CPersonalShop::GetPaymentJewelCount(LPOBJ lpObj,int* count,int* table,int type,int value) // OK
   167: {
   168: 	#if(GAMESERVER_UPDATE>=802)
   169: 
   170: 	value = (int)GetRoundValue((((float)value*(100-gServerInfo.m_PersonalShopJewelCommisionRate))/100));
   171: 
   172: 	#endif
   173: 
   174: 	table[3] = value/30;
   175: 	value = value%30;
   176: 
   177: 	table[2] = value/20;
   178: 	value = value%20;
   179: 
   180: 	table[1] = value/10;
   181: 	value = value%10;
   182: 
   183: 	table[0] = value;
   184: 	value = 0;
   185: 
   186: 	(*count) += table[0]+table[1]+table[2]+table[3];
   187: }

   189: void CPersonalShop::SetRequireJewelCount(LPOBJ lpObj,int* table,int type) // OK
   190: {
   191: 	switch(type)
   192: 	{
   193: 		case 0:
   194: 			gItemManager.DeleteInventoryItemCount(lpObj,GET_ITEM(14,13),0,table[0]);
   195: 			gItemManager.DeleteInventoryItemCount(lpObj,GET_ITEM(12,30),0,table[1]);
   196: 			gItemManager.DeleteInventoryItemCount(lpObj,GET_ITEM(12,30),1,table[2]);
   197: 			gItemManager.DeleteInventoryItemCount(lpObj,GET_ITEM(12,30),2,table[3]);
   198: 			break;
   199: 		case 1:
   200: 			gItemManager.DeleteInventoryItemCount(lpObj,GET_ITEM(14,14),0,table[0]);
   201: 			gItemManager.DeleteInventoryItemCount(lpObj,GET_ITEM(12,31),0,table[1]);
   202: 			gItemManager.DeleteInventoryItemCount(lpObj,GET_ITEM(12,31),1,table[2]);
   203: 			gItemManager.DeleteInventoryItemCount(lpObj,GET_ITEM(12,31),2,table[3]);
   204: 			break;
   205: 		case 2:
   206: 			gItemManager.DeleteInventoryItemCount(lpObj,GET_ITEM(12,15),0,table[0]);
   207: 			gItemManager.DeleteInventoryItemCount(lpObj,GET_ITEM(12,141),0,table[1]);
   208: 			gItemManager.DeleteInventoryItemCount(lpObj,GET_ITEM(12,141),1,table[2]);
   209: 			gItemManager.DeleteInventoryItemCount(lpObj,GET_ITEM(12,141),2,table[3]);
   210: 			break;
   211: 	}
   212: }
```

```
   281: void CPersonalShop::CGPShopSetItemPriceRecv(PMSG_PSHOP_SET_ITEM_PRICE_RECV* lpMsg,int aIndex) // OK
   282: {
   283: 	LPOBJ lpObj = &gObj[aIndex];
   284: 
   285: 	if(gObjIsConnectedGP(aIndex) == 0)
   286: 	{
   287: 		return;
   288: 	}
   289: 
   290: 	if(INVENTORY_RANGE(lpMsg->slot) == 0)
   291: 	{
   292: 		this->GCPShopSetItemPriceSend(aIndex,2,lpMsg->slot);
   293: 		return;
   294: 	}
   295: 
   296: 	if(lpObj->Inventory[lpMsg->slot].IsItem() == 0)
   297: 	{
   298: 		this->GCPShopSetItemPriceSend(aIndex,3,lpMsg->slot);
   299: 		return;
   300: 	}
   301: 
   302: 	int price = MAKE_NUMBERDW(MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->price[3],lpMsg->price[2]),MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->price[1],lpMsg->price[0]));
   303: 
   304: 	#if(GAMESERVER_UPDATE>=802)
   305: 
   306: 	int JoBPrice = MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->JoBPrice[1],lpMsg->JoBPrice[0]);
   307: 
   308: 	int JoSPrice = MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->JoSPrice[1],lpMsg->JoSPrice[0]);
   309: 
   310: 	int JoCPrice = MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->JoCPrice[1],lpMsg->JoCPrice[0]);
   311: 
   312: 	#endif
   313: 
   314: 	#if(GAMESERVER_UPDATE>=802)
   315: 
   316: 	if(price <= 0 && JoBPrice <= 0 && JoSPrice <= 0 && JoCPrice <= 0)
   317: 	{
   318: 		this->GCPShopSetItemPriceSend(aIndex,4,lpMsg->slot);
   319: 		return;
   320: 	}
   321: 
   322: 	#else
   323: 
   324: 	if(price <= 0)
   325: 	{
   326: 		this->GCPShopSetItemPriceSend(aIndex,4,lpMsg->slot);
   327: 		return;
   328: 	}
   329: 
   330: 	#endif
   331: 
   332: 	if(lpObj->Level <= 5)
   333: 	{
   334: 		this->GCPShopSetItemPriceSend(aIndex,5,lpMsg->slot);
   335: 		return;
   336: 	}
   337: 
   338: 	lpObj->Inventory[lpMsg->slot].m_PShopValue = price;
   339: 
   340: 	#if(GAMESERVER_UPDATE>=802)
   341: 
   342: 	lpObj->Inventory[lpMsg->slot].m_PShopJoBValue = JoBPrice;
   343: 
   344: 	lpObj->Inventory[lpMsg->slot].m_PShopJoSValue = JoSPrice;
   345: 
   346: 	lpObj->Inventory[lpMsg->slot].m_PShopJoCValue = JoCPrice;
   347: 
   348: 	#endif
   349: 
   350: 	this->GCPShopSetItemPriceSend(aIndex,1,lpMsg->slot);
   351: }
   352: 
   353: void CPersonalShop::CGPShopOpenRecv(PMSG_PSHOP_OPEN_RECV* lpMsg,int aIndex) // OK
   354: {
   355: 	if(gServerInfo.m_PersonalShopSwitch == 0)
   356: 	{
   357: 		return;
   358: 	}
   359: 
   360: 	if(gCustomStore.OnPShopOpen(&gObj[aIndex]) != 0)
   361: 	{
   362: 		return;
   363: 	}
   364: 
   365: 	LPOBJ lpObj = &gObj[aIndex];
   366: 
   367: 	if(gObjIsConnectedGP(aIndex) == 0)
   368: 	{
   369: 		return;
   370: 	}
   371: 
   372: 	if(lpObj->Interface.use != 0 && lpObj->Interface.type != INTERFACE_PERSONAL_SHOP)
   373: 	{
   374: 		return;
   375: 	}
   376: 
   377: 	if(lpObj->TradeDuel != 0)
   378: 	{
   379: 		return;
   380: 	}
   381: 
   382: 	if(CA_MAP_RANGE(lpObj->Map) != 0 || CC_MAP_RANGE(lpObj->Map) != 0 || IT_MAP_RANGE(lpObj->Map) != 0)
   383: 	{
   384: 		this->GCPShopOpenSend(aIndex,0);
   385: 		return;
   386: 	}
   387: 
   388: 	if(this->CheckPersonalShopOpen(aIndex) == 0)
   389: 	{
   390: 		this->GCPShopOpenSend(aIndex,0);
   391: 		return;
   392: 	}
   393: 
   394: 	if(lpObj->Level <= 5)
   395: 	{
   396: 		this->GCPShopOpenSend(aIndex,2);
   397: 		return;
   398: 	}
   399: 
   400: 	if(lpObj->PShopOpen == 0)
   401: 	{
   402: 		lpObj->PShopOpen = 1;
   403: 		memcpy(lpObj->PShopText,lpMsg->text,sizeof(lpMsg->text));
   404: 	}
   405: 	else
   406: 	{
   407: 		memcpy(lpObj->PShopText,lpMsg->text,sizeof(lpMsg->text));
   408: 		this->GCPShopTextChangeSend(aIndex);
   409: 	}
   410: 
   411: 	this->GCPShopOpenSend(aIndex,1);
   412: }
   413:

   434: void CPersonalShop::CGPShopItemListRecv(PMSG_PSHOP_ITEM_LIST_RECV* lpMsg,int aIndex) // OK
   435: {
   436: 	LPOBJ lpObj = &gObj[aIndex];
   437: 
   438: 	if(gObjIsConnectedGP(aIndex) == 0)
   439: 	{
   440: 		return;
   441: 	}
   442: 
   443: 	int bIndex = MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->index[0],lpMsg->index[1]);
   444: 
   445: 	if(gObjIsConnectedGP(bIndex) == 0)
   446: 	{
   447: 		return;
   448: 	}
   449: 
   450: 	LPOBJ lpTarget = &gObj[bIndex];
   451: 
   452: 	if(lpTarget->PShopOpen == 0)
   453: 	{
   454: 		this->GCPShopItemListSend(aIndex,-1,3,0);
   455: 		return;
   456: 	}
   457: 
   458: 	char name[11] = {0};
   459: 
   460: 	memcpy(name,lpMsg->name,sizeof(lpMsg->name));
   461: 
   462: 	if(strcmp(name,lpTarget->Name) != 0)
   463: 	{
   464: 		this->GCPShopItemListSend(aIndex,-1,2,0);
   465: 		return;
   466: 	}
   467: 
   468: 	gCustomStore.GCOffTradeSend(lpObj->Index,lpTarget->Index);
   469: 
   470: 	lpObj->PShopWantDeal = 1;
   471: 	lpObj->PShopDealerIndex = bIndex;
   472: 	memcpy(lpObj->PShopDealerName,lpMsg->name,sizeof(lpObj->PShopDealerName));
   473: 	this->GCPShopItemListSend(aIndex,bIndex,1,0);
   474: 
   475: 	gCustomStore.OnPShopItemList(lpObj,lpTarget);
   476: 
   477: 
   478: }
```

```
    33: int CWarehouse::GetWarehouseTaxMoney(int level,int lock) // OK
    34: {
    35: 	int tax = (int)(((level*level)*0.1)*0.4);
    36: 
    37: 	if(lock != 0)
    38: 	{
    39: 		tax += (level*2);
    40: 	}
    41: 
    42: 	if(tax <= 0)
    43: 	{
    44: 		tax = 1;
    45: 	}
    46: 
    47: 	if(tax >= 1000)
    48: 	{
    49: 		tax = (tax/100)*100;
    50: 	}
    51: 	else if(tax >= 100)
    52: 	{
    53: 		tax = (tax/10)*10;
    54: 	}
    55: 
    56: 	return tax;
    57: }

    59: void CWarehouse::CGWarehouseMoneyRecv(PMSG_WAREHOUSE_MONEY_RECV* lpMsg,int aIndex) // OK
    60: {

    65: 		return;
    66: 	}
    67: 
    68: 	if(lpObj->Interface.use == 0 || lpObj->Interface.type != INTERFACE_WAREHOUSE || lpObj->Interface.state == 0)

    70: 		return;
    71: 	}
    72: 
    73: 	if(lpMsg->type != 0 && lpMsg->type != 1)
    74: 	{
    75: 		return;
    76: 	}
    77:

    79: 	{
    80: 		if(lpMsg->money <= 0 || lpMsg->money > MAX_WAREHOUSE_MONEY)

    82: 			this->GCWarehouseMoneySend(aIndex,0,0,0);
    83: 			return;
    84: 		}
    85: 
    86: 		if((lpObj->WarehouseMoney+lpMsg->money) > MAX_WAREHOUSE_MONEY)

    88: 			this->GCWarehouseMoneySend(aIndex,0,0,0);
    89: 			return;
    90: 		}
    91:

    94: 			this->GCWarehouseMoneySend(aIndex,0,0,0);
    95: 			return;
    96: 		}
    97: 
    98: 		lpObj->Money -= lpMsg->money;
```

```
   264: {
   265: 	BYTE send[4096];
   266: 
   267: 	PMSG_SHOP_ITEM_LIST_SEND pMsg;
   268: 
   269: 	pMsg.header.set(0x31,0);
   270: 
   271: 	int size = sizeof(pMsg);
   272: 
   273: 	pMsg.type = 0;
   274: 
   275: 	pMsg.count = 0;
   276: 
   277: 	PMSG_SHOP_ITEM_LIST info;
   278: 
   279: 	for(int n=0;n < WAREHOUSE_SIZE;n++)
   280: 	{
   281: 		if(lpObj->Warehouse[n].IsItem() == 0)
   282: 		{
   283: 			continue;
   284: 		}
   285: 
   286: 		info.slot = n;
   287: 
   288: 		gItemManager.ItemByteConvert(info.ItemInfo,lpObj->Warehouse[n]);
   289: 
   290: 		memcpy(&send[size],&info,sizeof(info));
   291: 		size += sizeof(info);
   292: 
   293: 		pMsg.count++;
   294: 	}
   295: 
   296: 	pMsg.header.size[0] = SET_NUMBERHB(size);
   297: 	pMsg.header.size[1] = SET_NUMBERLB(size);
   298: 
   299: 	memcpy(send,&pMsg,sizeof(pMsg));
   300: 
   301: 	DataSend(lpObj->Index,send,size);
   302: 
   303: 	this->GCWarehouseMoneySend(lpObj->Index,1,lpObj->Money,lpObj->WarehouseMoney);
   304: }

   332: void CWarehouse::DGWarehouseItemRecv(SDHP_WAREHOUSE_ITEM_RECV* lpMsg) // OK
   333: {
   334: 	if(gObjIsAccountValid(lpMsg->index,lpMsg->account) == 0)
   335: 	{
   336: 		LogAdd(LOG_RED,"[DGWarehouseItemRecv] Invalid Account [%d](%s)",lpMsg->index,lpMsg->account);
   337: 		CloseClient(lpMsg->index);
   338: 		return;
   339: 	}
   340: 
   341: 	LPOBJ lpObj = &gObj[lpMsg->index];
   342: 
   343: 	if(lpObj->Interface.use == 0 || lpObj->Interface.type != INTERFACE_WAREHOUSE || lpObj->Interface.state != 0)
   344: 	{
   345: 		return;
   346: 	}
   347: 
   348: 	if(lpObj->LoadWarehouse != 0)
   349: 	{
   350: 		return;
   351: 	}
   352: 
   353: 	memset(lpObj->WarehouseMap,0xFF,WAREHOUSE_SIZE);
   354: 
   355: 	for(int n=0;n < WAREHOUSE_SIZE;n++)
   356: 	{
   357: 		CItem item;
   358: 
   359: 		lpObj->Warehouse[n].Clear();
   360: 
   361: 		if(gItemManager.ConvertItemByte(&item,lpMsg->WarehouseItem[n]) != 0){gItemManager.WarehouseAddItem(lpObj->Index,item,n);}
   362: 	}
   363: 
   364: 	lpObj->WarehouseMoney = lpMsg->WarehouseMoney;
   365: 
   366: 	lpObj->WarehousePW = lpMsg->WarehousePassword;
   367: 
   368: 	PMSG_NPC_TALK_SEND pMsg;
   369: 
   370: 	pMsg.header.set(0x30,sizeof(pMsg));
   371: 
   372: 	pMsg.result = 2;
   373: 
   374: 	DataSend(lpObj->Index,(BYTE*)&pMsg,pMsg.header.size);
   375: 
   376: 	gWarehouse.GCWarehouseListSend(lpObj);
   377: 
   378: 	gWarehouse.GCWarehouseStateSend(lpObj->Index,((lpObj->WarehousePW>0)?((lpObj->WarehouseLock==0)?12:1):0));
   379: 
   380: 	lpObj->Interface.use = 1;
   381: 
   382: 	lpObj->Interface.type = INTERFACE_WAREHOUSE;
   383: 
   384: 	lpObj->Interface.state = 1;
   385: 
   386: 	lpObj->LoadWarehouse = 1;
   387: 
   388: 	GDPetItemInfoSend(lpObj->Index,1);
   389: }
```

```
   484:
```

```
   124: void C380ItemOption::Calc380ItemOption(LPOBJ lpObj,bool flag) // OK
   125: {
   126: 	for(int n=0;n < INVENTORY_WEAR_SIZE;n++)
   127: 	{
   128: 		if(lpObj->Inventory[n].IsItem() == 0 || lpObj->Inventory[n].m_IsValidItem == 0 || lpObj->Inventory[n].Is380Item() == 0)
   129: 		{
   130: 			continue;
   131: 		}
   132: 
   133: 		for(int i=0;i < MAX_380_ITEM_OPTION_INDEX;i++)
   134: 		{
   135: 			int ItemOption = g380ItemType.Get380ItemOptionIndex(lpObj->Inventory[n].m_Index,i);
   136: 
   137: 			if(ItemOption == ITEM_380_OPTION_NONE)
   138: 			{
   139: 				continue;
   140: 			}
   141: 
   142: 			ITEM_380_OPTION_INFO* lpInfo = this->GetInfo(ItemOption);
   143: 
   144: 			if(lpInfo == 0)
   145: 			{
   146: 				continue;
   147: 			}
   148: 
   149: 			int OptionValue = g380ItemType.Get380ItemOptionValue(lpObj->Inventory[n].m_Index,i);
   150: 
   151: 			OptionValue = ((OptionValue==-1)?lpInfo->Value:OptionValue);
   152: 
   153: 			this->InsertOption(lpObj,lpInfo->Index,OptionValue,flag);
   154: 		}
   155: 	}
   156: }
   157: 
   158: void C380ItemOption::InsertOption(LPOBJ lpObj,int index,int value,bool flag) // OK
   159: {
   160: 	if(flag != 0)
   161: 	{
   162: 		return;
   163: 	}
   164: 
   165: 	switch(index)
   166: 	{
   167: 		case ITEM_380_OPTION_ADD_ATTACK_SUCCESS_RATE_PVP:
   168: 			lpObj->AttackSuccessRatePvP += value;
   169: 			break;
   170: 		case ITEM_380_OPTION_ADD_DAMAGE_PVP:
   171: 			lpObj->DamagePvP += value;
   172: 			break;
   173: 		case ITEM_380_OPTION_ADD_DEFENSE_SUCCESS_RATE_PVP:
   174: 			lpObj->DefenseSuccessRatePvP += value;
   175: 			break;
   176: 		case ITEM_380_OPTION_ADD_DEFENSE_PVP:
   177: 			lpObj->DefensePvP += value;
   178: 			break;
   179: 		case ITEM_380_OPTION_ADD_MAX_HP:
   180: 			lpObj->AddLife += value;
   181: 			break;
   182: 		case ITEM_380_OPTION_ADD_MAX_SD:
   183: 			lpObj->AddShield += value;
   184: 			break;
   185: 		case ITEM_380_OPTION_SET_SD_RECOVERY_TYPE:
   186: 			lpObj->SDRecoveryType = value;
   187: 			break;
   188: 		case ITEM_380_OPTION_ADD_SD_RECOVERY_RATE:
   189: 			lpObj->SDRecoveryRate += value;
   190: 			break;
   191: 	}
   192: }
```

```
   143: void FriendListResult(LPBYTE lpMsg)
   144: {
   145: 	FHP_FRIENDLIST_COUNT * lpCount;
   146: 	FHP_FRIENDLIST * lpList;
   147: 	int lOfs = sizeof(FHP_FRIENDLIST_COUNT);
   148: 	PMSG_FRIEND_LIST_COUNT pCount;
   149: 	PMSG_FRIEND_LIST pList;
   150: 	int pOfs = sizeof(PMSG_FRIEND_LIST_COUNT);
   151: 
   152: 	lpCount = (FHP_FRIENDLIST_COUNT *)lpMsg;
   153: 
   154: 	BYTE TmpSendBuf[2000];
   155: 
   156: 	if ( !gObjIsNameValid(lpCount->Number, lpCount->Name))
   157: 	{

   159: 	}
   160: 
   161: 	pCount.Count = lpCount->Count;

   165: 	if ( pCount.Count != 0 )
   166: 	{
   167: 		for ( int n=0;n<pCount.Count;n++)
   168: 		{
   169: 			lpList = (FHP_FRIENDLIST *)&lpMsg[lOfs];
   170:

   178: 			pOfs+=sizeof(PMSG_FRIEND_LIST);

   235: void FriendAddRequest(PMSG_FRIEND_ADD_REQ * lpMsg, int aIndex)
   236: {
   237: 	if ( !gObjIsConnectedGP(aIndex))
   238: 	{
   239: 		return;
   240: 	}
   241: 
   242: 	//Season 4.5 Addon start
   243: 	LPOBJ find = gObjFind(lpMsg->Name);
   244: 
   245: 	int aTargetIndex = ((find==0)?-1:find->Index);
   246: 
   247:     if(aTargetIndex >= 0 && aTargetIndex <= MAX_OBJECT-1 && gObj[aTargetIndex].Type != 1  )
   248:     {
   249: 		CloseClient(aIndex);
   250: 		return;
   251: 	}
   252: 	//Season 4.5 Addon end
   253: 
   254: 	FHP_FRIEND_ADD_REQ pMsg;
   255: 
   256: 	pMsg.h.set(0x63, sizeof(pMsg));
   257: 	memcpy(pMsg.Name, gObj[aIndex].Name, sizeof(pMsg.Name));
   258: 	memset(pMsg.FriendName, 0 , sizeof(pMsg.FriendName));
   259: 	memcpy(pMsg.FriendName, lpMsg->Name, sizeof(lpMsg->Name));
   260: 	pMsg.Number = aIndex;
   261: 
   262: 	char szName[11];
   263: 
   264: 	memset(szName,0,sizeof(szName));
   265: 
   266: 	memcpy(szName,lpMsg->Name,sizeof(lpMsg->Name));
   267: 
   268: 	if ( !strcmp(gObj[aIndex].Name, szName))
   269: 	{
   270: 		PMSG_FRIEND_ADD_RESULT pResult;
   271: 
   272: 		pResult.h.set(0xC1, sizeof(pResult));
   273: 		memcpy(pResult.Name, lpMsg->Name, sizeof(pMsg.Name));
   274: 		pResult.Result = 5;
   275: 		pResult.State = -1;
   276: 
   277: 		DataSend(aIndex, (LPBYTE)&pResult, sizeof(pResult));
   278: 	}
   279: 	else
   280: 	{
   281: 		CSDataSend((BYTE*)&pMsg, pMsg.h.size);
   282: 	}
   283: }
```

```
    81: void ChatServerProtocolCore(BYTE head,BYTE* lpMsg,int size) // OK
    82: {

    84: 
    85: 	switch(head)

    87: 		case 0x60:

    98: 			break;

   126: void FriendListRequest(int aIndex)
   127: {
   128: 	if ( gObjIsConnectedGP(aIndex) == FALSE )
   129: 	{
   130: 		return;
   131: 	}
   132: 
   133: 	FHP_FRIENDLIST_REQ pMsg;
   134: 
   135: 	pMsg.h.set(0x60, sizeof(FHP_FRIENDLIST_REQ));
   136: 	memcpy(pMsg.Name, gObj[aIndex].Name, sizeof(pMsg.Name));
   137: 	pMsg.Number = aIndex;
   138: 	pMsg.pServer = (BYTE)gServerInfo.m_ServerCode;
   139: 
   140: 	CSDataSend((BYTE*)&pMsg, pMsg.h.size);
   141: }
   142: 
   143: void FriendListResult(LPBYTE lpMsg)
   144: {
   145: 	FHP_FRIENDLIST_COUNT * lpCount;
   146: 	FHP_FRIENDLIST * lpList;
   147: 	int lOfs = sizeof(FHP_FRIENDLIST_COUNT);
   148: 	PMSG_FRIEND_LIST_COUNT pCount;
   149: 	PMSG_FRIEND_LIST pList;
   150: 	int pOfs = sizeof(PMSG_FRIEND_LIST_COUNT);
   151: 
   152: 	lpCount = (FHP_FRIENDLIST_COUNT *)lpMsg;
   153: 
   154: 	BYTE TmpSendBuf[2000];
   155: 
   156: 	if ( !gObjIsNameValid(lpCount->Number, lpCount->Name))
   157: 	{
   158: 		return;
   159: 	}
   160: 
   161: 	pCount.Count = lpCount->Count;
   162: 	pCount.MemoCount = lpCount->MailCount;
   163: 	pCount.MailTotal = 50;
   164: 
   165: 	if ( pCount.Count != 0 )
   166: 	{
   167: 		for ( int n=0;n<pCount.Count;n++)
   168: 		{
   169: 			lpList = (FHP_FRIENDLIST *)&lpMsg[lOfs];
   170: 
   171: 			memcpy(pList.Name,lpList->Name,sizeof(pList.Name));
   172: 
   173: 			pList.Server = lpList->Server;
   174: 
   175: 			memcpy(&TmpSendBuf[pOfs], &pList, sizeof(PMSG_FRIEND_LIST));
   176: 
   177: 			lOfs+=sizeof(FHP_FRIENDLIST);
   178: 			pOfs+=sizeof(PMSG_FRIEND_LIST);
   179: 		}
   180: 	}
   181: 
   182: 	pCount.h.set(0xC0, pOfs);
   183: 	memcpy(TmpSendBuf, &pCount, sizeof(PMSG_FRIEND_LIST_COUNT));
   184: 
   185: 	DataSend(lpCount->Number, TmpSendBuf, pOfs);
   186: }
   187: 
   188: void WaitFriendListResult(FHP_WAITFRIENDLIST_COUNT * lpMsg)
   189: {
   190: 	PMSG_FRIEND_ADD_SIN_REQ pMsg;
   191: 
   192: 	if ( !gObjIsNameValid(lpMsg->Number, lpMsg->Name))
   193: 	{
   194: 		return;
   195: 	}
   196: 
   197: 	pMsg.h.set(0xC2, sizeof(pMsg));
   198: 	memcpy(pMsg.Name, lpMsg->FriendName, sizeof(pMsg.Name));
   199: 
   200: 	DataSend(lpMsg->Number, (LPBYTE)&pMsg, sizeof(pMsg));
   201: }
```

```
   390: 	}
   391: 
   392: 	if(lpMsg->OpenType == 0)

   394: 		if(lpObj->Interface.use == 0)

   396: 			lpObj->Interface.use = 1;
   397: 			lpObj->Interface.type = INTERFACE_CASH_SHOP;
   398: 			lpObj->Interface.state = 0;
   399:

   401:

   403: 		}
   404: 		else
   405: 		{
   406: 			pMsg.result = 8;
   407: 
   408: 			DataSend(aIndex,(BYTE*)&pMsg,pMsg.header.size);
   409: 		}
   410: 	}
   411: 	else
   412: 	{
   413: 		lpObj->Interface.use = 0;
   414: 		lpObj->Interface.type = INTERFACE_NONE;
   415: 		lpObj->Interface.state = 0;
   416: 
   417: 		pMsg.result = 0;
   418: 
   419: 		DataSend(aIndex,(BYTE*)&pMsg,pMsg.header.size);
```

```
   365: void CCashShop::CGCashShopOpenRecv(PMSG_CASH_SHOP_OPEN_RECV* lpMsg,int aIndex) // OK
   366: {
   367: 	if(gServerInfo.m_CashShopSwitch == 0)

   369: 		return;
   370: 	}
   371:

   373: 
   374: 	PMSG_CASH_SHOP_OPEN_SEND pMsg;
   375:

   377: 
   378: 	if(gObjIsConnectedGP(aIndex) == 0)
   379: 	{

   381: 		DataSend(aIndex,(BYTE*)&pMsg,pMsg.header.size);

   392: 	if(lpMsg->OpenType == 0)
   393: 	{
   394: 		if(lpObj->Interface.use == 0)

   397: 			lpObj->Interface.type = INTERFACE_CASH_SHOP;

   416:
```

```
   592: void CCashShop::GCCashShopInitSend(LPOBJ lpObj) // OK
   593: {
   594: 	if(gServerInfo.m_CashShopSwitch == 0)
   595: 	{
   596: 		return;
   597: 	}
   598: 
   599: 	PSBMSG_HEAD pMsg;
   600: 
   601: 	pMsg.set(0xD2,0x00,sizeof(pMsg));
   602: 
   603: 	DataSend(lpObj->Index,(BYTE*)&pMsg,pMsg.size);
   604: 
   605: 	this->GCCashShopScriptVersionSend(lpObj);
   606: 
   607: 	this->GCCashShopBannerVersionSend(lpObj);
   608: }
   609: 
   610: void CCashShop::GCCashShopScriptVersionSend(LPOBJ lpObj) // OK
   611: {
   612: 	PMSG_CASH_SHOP_VERSION_SEND pMsg;
   613: 
   614: 	pMsg.header.set(0xD2,0x0C,sizeof(pMsg));
   615: 
   616: 	pMsg.version[0] = gServerInfo.m_CashShopScriptVersion1;
   617: 
   618: 	pMsg.version[1] = gServerInfo.m_CashShopScriptVersion2;
   619: 
   620: 	pMsg.version[2] = gServerInfo.m_CashShopScriptVersion3;
   621: 
   622: 	DataSend(lpObj->Index,(BYTE*)&pMsg,pMsg.header.size);
   623: }
   624: 
   625: void CCashShop::GCCashShopBannerVersionSend(LPOBJ lpObj) // OK
   626: {
   627: 	PMSG_CASH_SHOP_VERSION_SEND pMsg;
   628: 
   629: 	pMsg.header.set(0xD2,0x15,sizeof(pMsg));
   630: 
   631: 	pMsg.version[0] = gServerInfo.m_CashShopBannerVersion1;
   632: 
   633: 	pMsg.version[1] = gServerInfo.m_CashShopBannerVersion2;
   634: 
   635: 	pMsg.version[2] = gServerInfo.m_CashShopBannerVersion3;

   640: void CCashShop::GCCashShopItemSend(LPOBJ lpObj,int PageCount,CASH_SHOP_INVENTORY* CashInventory) // OK
   641: {
   642: 	PMSG_CASH_SHOP_ITEM_SEND pMsg;
   643: 
   644: 	pMsg.header.set(0xD2,0x0D,sizeof(pMsg));
   645: 
   646: 	for(int n=0;n < PageCount;n++)
   647: 	{
   648: 		pMsg.BaseItemCode = CashInventory[n].BaseItemCode;
   649: 
   650: 		pMsg.MainItemCode = CashInventory[n].MainItemCode;
   651: 
   652: 		pMsg.PackageMainIndex = CashInventory[n].PackageMainIndex;
   653: 
   654: 		pMsg.ProductBaseIndex = CashInventory[n].ProductBaseIndex;
   655: 
   656: 		pMsg.ProductMainIndex = CashInventory[n].ProductMainIndex;
   657: 
   658: 		pMsg.CoinValue = CashInventory[n].CoinValue;
   659: 
   660: 		pMsg.ProductType = CashInventory[n].ProductType;
   661: 
   662: 		DataSend(lpObj->Index,(BYTE*)&pMsg,pMsg.header.size);
   663: 	}
   664: }
```

```
   192: void CChaosCastle::MainProc() // OK
   193: {
   194: 	for(int n=0;n < MAX_CC_LEVEL;n++)

   197:

   199: 
   200: 		if(elapsed < 1000)
   201: 		{
   202: 			continue;
   203: 		}
   204: 
   205: 		lpLevel->TickCount = GetTickCount();
   206: 
   207: 		lpLevel->RemainTime = (int)difftime(lpLevel->TargetTime,time(0));

   213: 				if (gServerDisplayer.EventCc != -1)

   215: 					gServerDisplayer.EventCc = -1;
   216: 				}

   219: 			{
   220: 				if (lpLevel->State == CC_STATE_EMPTY)

   222: 					gServerDisplayer.EventCc = lpLevel->RemainTime;

   225: 				{
   226: 					if (gServerDisplayer.EventCc != 0)
   227: 					{
   228: 						gServerDisplayer.EventCc = 0;
   229: 					}
   230: 				}
   231: 			}
   232: 		}
   233: 
   234: 		if(gServerDisplayer.EventCc > 0)
   235: 		{
   236: 			if (lpLevel->State != CC_STATE_EMPTY && lpLevel->State != CC_STATE_BLANK)
   237: 			{
   238: 				gServerDisplayer.EventCc = 0;
   239: 			}
   240: 		}
   241: 
   242: 		switch(lpLevel->State)
   243: 		{
   244: 			case CC_STATE_BLANK:
   245: 				this->ProcState_BLANK(lpLevel);
   246: 				break;
   247: 			case CC_STATE_EMPTY:
   248: 				this->ProcState_EMPTY(lpLevel);
   249: 				break;
   250: 			case CC_STATE_STAND:
   251: 				this->ProcState_STAND(lpLevel);
   252: 				break;
   253: 			case CC_STATE_START:
   254: 				this->ProcState_START(lpLevel);
   255: 				break;

   257: 				this->ProcState_CLEAN(lpLevel);
   258: 				break;
   259: 		}

   261: 		this->CheckDelayScore(lpLevel);
```

```
   206: 	int ItemOK;
   207: 	int Qtd;
   208: 
   209: 	for(std::vector<CUSTOM_MIX_ITEM_INFO>::iterator it=this->m_CustomMixItemInfo.begin();it != this->m_CustomMixItemInfo.end();it++)
   210: 	{
   211: 		if (it->Index <= 0)
   212: 		{
   213: 			continue;
   214: 		}
   215: 
   216: 		if (it->Index != index)
   217: 		{
   218: 			continue;
   219: 		}
   220: 
   221: 		ItemOK	= 0;
   222: 		Qtd		= 0;
   223: 
   224: 		for(int n=0;n < CHAOS_BOX_SIZE;n++)
   225: 		{
   226: 			if(lpObj->ChaosBox[n].IsItem() == 0)
   227: 			{
   228: 				continue;
   229: 			}
   230: 
   231: 			if( lpObj->ChaosBox[n].m_Index == it->ItemIndex)
   232: 			{
   233: 				ItemOK	= 0;
   234: 				if (it->Level >= 0)
   235: 				{
   236: 					if(lpObj->ChaosBox[n].m_Level == it->Level)
   237: 					{
   238: 						ItemOK++;
   239: 					}
   240: 				}
   241: 				else
   242: 				{
   243: 					ItemOK++;
   244: 				}
   245: 
   246: 				if (it->Option1 >= 0 )
   247: 				{
   248: 					if (lpObj->ChaosBox[n].m_Option1 == it->Option1)
   249: 					{
   250: 						ItemOK++;
   251: 					}
   252: 				}
   253: 				else
   254: 				{
   255: 					ItemOK++;
   256: 				}
   257: 
   258: 				if (it->Option2 >= 0)
   259: 				{
   260: 					if (lpObj->ChaosBox[n].m_Option2 == it->Option2)
   261: 					{
   262: 						ItemOK++;
   263: 					}
   264: 				}
   265: 				else
   266: 				{
   267: 					ItemOK++;
   268: 				}
   269: 
   270: 				if (it->Option3 >= 0)
   271: 				{
   272: 					if (lpObj->ChaosBox[n].m_Option3 == it->Option3)
   273: 					{
   274: 						ItemOK++;
   275: 					}
   276: 				}
   277: 				else
   278: 				{
   279: 					ItemOK++;
   280: 				}
   281: 
   282: 				if (it->NewOption >= 0)
   283: 				{
   284: 					if (GetNewOptionCount(lpObj->ChaosBox[n].m_NewOption) >= it->NewOption)
   285: 					{
   286: 						ItemOK++;
   287: 					}
   288: 				}
   289: 				else
   290: 				{
   291: 					ItemOK++;
   292: 				}
   293: 
   294: 				if (it->SetOption >= 0)
   295: 				{
   296: 					if (lpObj->ChaosBox[n].IsSetItem())
   297: 					{
   298: 						ItemOK++;
   299: 					}
   300: 				}
   301: 				else
   302: 				{
   303: 					ItemOK++;
   304: 				}
   305: 
   306: 				if (ItemOK >= 6)
   307: 				{
   308: 					Qtd++;
   309: 				}
   310: 
   311: 			}
   312: 		}
   313: 
   314: 		if (Qtd < it->Count)
   315: 		{
   316: 			return 0;
   317: 		}
   318: 	}
   319: 	return 1;
```

```
   182: void CCustomStartItem::InsertItem(LPOBJ lpObj) // OK
   183: {
   184: 
   185: 	for(int n=0;n < INVENTORY_WEAR_SIZE;n++)
   186: 	{
   187: 		for(std::vector<STARTITEM_ITEM_INFO>::iterator it=this->m_CustomStartItemItemInfo.begin();it != this->m_CustomStartItemItemInfo.end();it++)
   188: 		{
   189: 			if (it->Class != lpObj->Class)
   190: 			{
   191: 				continue;
   192: 			}
   193: 			if (it->Slot != n)
   194: 			{
   195: 				continue;
   196: 			}
   197: 
   198: 
   199: 			BYTE ItemSocketOption[MAX_SOCKET_OPTION] = {0xFF,0xFF,0xFF,0xFF,0xFF};
   200: 
   201: 			if (gSocketItemType.CheckSocketItemType(it->ItemIndex) == 1)
   202: 			{
   203: 				int qtd = gSocketItemType.GetSocketItemMaxSocket(it->ItemIndex);

   222: void CCustomStartItem::InsertBuff(LPOBJ lpObj) // OK

   224: 	for(std::vector<STARTITEM_BUFF_INFO>::iterator it=this->m_CustomStartItemBuffInfo.begin();it != this->m_CustomStartItemBuffInfo.end();it++)

   235: 			continue;

   242: 
   243: void CCustomStartItem::InsertCoin(LPOBJ lpObj) // OK
   244: {

   246: 	{

   248: 		{
   249: 			continue;
   250: 		}
   251: 
   252: 		GDSetCoinSend(lpObj->Index, (it->Coin1 < 0)?0:it->Coin1, (it->Coin2 < 0)?0:it->Coin2, (it->Coin3 < 0)?0:it->Coin3,"StartItem");
   253: 	}
   254: }
   255: 
   256: void CCustomStartItem::DGStartItemSaveSend(LPOBJ lpObj) // OK
   257: {
   258: 	SDHP_STARTITEM_SAVE_SEND pMsg;
   259: 
   260: 	pMsg.header.set(0xF7,0x03,sizeof(pMsg));
   261: 
   262: 	pMsg.index = lpObj->Index;
   263: 
   264: 	memcpy(pMsg.name,lpObj->Name,sizeof(pMsg.name));
   265: 
   266: 	gDataServerConnection.DataSend((BYTE*)&pMsg,sizeof(pMsg));
   267: }
```

```
   281: void GDGuildDestroySend(int aIndex, LPSTR Name, LPSTR Master)
   282: {
   283: 	SDHP_GUILDDESTROY pMsg;
   284: 
   285: 	pMsg.h.type = 0xC1;
   286: 	pMsg.h.head = 0x31;
   287: 	pMsg.h.size = sizeof(pMsg);
   288: 	pMsg.NumberH = SET_NUMBERHB(aIndex);
   289: 	pMsg.NumberL = SET_NUMBERLB(aIndex);
   290: 	memcpy(pMsg.Master, Master, sizeof(pMsg.Master));
   291: 	memcpy(pMsg.GuildName, Name, sizeof(pMsg.GuildName));
   292: 
   293: 	ESDataSend((BYTE*)&pMsg, pMsg.h.size);
   294: }
   295: 
   296: void GDGuildDestroyRecv(SDHP_GUILDDESTROY_RESULT * lpMsg)
   297: {
   298: 	int aIndex = -1;
   299: 	char szMaster[11];
   300: 	char szGuildName[9];
   301: 
   302: 	memcpy(szMaster, lpMsg->Master, sizeof(szMaster));
   303: 	memcpy(szGuildName, lpMsg->GuildName, sizeof(szGuildName));
   304: 
   305: 	if ( lpMsg->Flag == 1 )
   306: 	{
   307: 		aIndex = MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->NumberH, lpMsg->NumberL);
   308: 
   309: 		if ( gObjIsConnected(aIndex) == TRUE )
   310: 		{
   311: 			if ( szMaster[0] == gObj[aIndex].Name[0] )
   312: 			{
   313: 				if ( strcmp(szMaster, gObj[aIndex].Name) == 0 )
   314: 				{
   315: 					GCResultSend(aIndex, 0x53, lpMsg->Result);
   316: 
   317: 					if ( lpMsg->Result == 1 || lpMsg->Result == 4 )
   318: 					{
   319: 						GCGuildViewportDelNow(aIndex, TRUE);
   320: 					}
   321: 				}
   322: 			}
   323: 		}
   324: 	}
   325: 
   326: 	if ( lpMsg->Result == 1 || lpMsg->Result == 4)
   327: 	{
   328: 		GUILD_INFO_STRUCT * lpGuild = gGuildClass.SearchGuild(szGuildName);
   329: 
   330: 		if ( lpGuild == NULL )
   331: 		{
   332: 			return;
   333: 		}
   334: 
   335: 		GUILD_INFO_STRUCT * lpRival = gGuildClass.SearchGuild_Number(lpGuild->GuildRival);
   336: 
   337: 		if ( lpRival != NULL )
   338: 		{
   339: 			lpRival->GuildRival = 0;
   340: 			lpRival->GuildRivalName[0] = 0;
   341: 		}
   342: 
   343: 		lpGuild->GuildUnion = 0;
   344: 		lpGuild->GuildRival = 0;
   345: 
   346: 		for ( int n=0;n<MAX_GUILD_USER;n++)
   347: 		{
   348: 			int iGuildMemberIndex = lpGuild->Index[n];
   349: 
   350: 			if ( lpGuild->Use[n] > 0 && iGuildMemberIndex != -1 )
   351: 			{
   352: 				LPOBJ lpObj = &gObj[iGuildMemberIndex];
   353: 
   354: 				if ( lpObj == NULL )
   355: 				{
   356: 					continue;
   357: 				}
   358: 
   359: 				if ( gObjIsConnected(iGuildMemberIndex) == FALSE )
   360: 				{
   361: 					continue;
   362: 				}
   363: 
   364: 				gObjNotifyUpdateUnionV1(lpObj);
   365: 				gObjNotifyUpdateUnionV2(lpObj);
   366: 				lpObj->Guild = NULL;
   367: 				lpObj->GuildNumber = 0;
   368: 				lpObj->GuildStatus = -1;
   369: 				lpObj->GuildUnionTimeStamp = 0;
   370: 				memset(lpObj->GuildName,0,sizeof(lpObj->GuildName));
   371: 
   372: 				GCResultSend(n,0x53, 1);
   373: 				GCGuildViewportDelNow(lpObj->Index, FALSE);
   374: 			}
   375: 		}
   376: 
   377: 		gUnionManager.DelUnion(lpGuild->Number);
   378: 		gGuildClass.DeleteGuild(szGuildName, szMaster);
   379: 	}
   380: }

   382: void GDGuildMemberAdd(int aIndex, LPSTR Name, LPSTR MemberId)
   383: {
   384: 	SDHP_GUILDMEMBERADD pMsg;
   385: 
   386: 	pMsg.h.type = 0xC1;
   387: 	pMsg.h.head = 0x32;
   388: 	pMsg.h.size = sizeof(pMsg);
   389: 	pMsg.NumberH = SET_NUMBERHB(aIndex);
   390: 	pMsg.NumberL = SET_NUMBERLB(aIndex);
   391: 	memcpy(pMsg.MemberID, MemberId, sizeof(pMsg.MemberID));
   392: 	memcpy(pMsg.GuildName, Name, sizeof(pMsg.GuildName));
   393: 
   394: 	ESDataSend((BYTE*)&pMsg, pMsg.h.size);
   395: }
   396: 
   397: void GDGuildMemberAddResult(SDHP_GUILDMEMBERADD_RESULT * lpMsg)
   398: {
   399: 	GUILD_INFO_STRUCT * lpNode;
   400: 	int aIndex = -1;
   401: 	int HereUserIndex=-1;
   402: 	char szMember[11];
   403: 	char szGuildName[9];
   404: 
   405: 	memcpy(szMember, lpMsg->MemberID, sizeof(szMember));
   406: 	memcpy(szGuildName, lpMsg->GuildName, sizeof(szGuildName));
   407: 
   408: 	if ( lpMsg->Flag == 1 )
   409: 	{
   410: 		aIndex = MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->NumberH, lpMsg->NumberL);
   411: 
   412: 		if ( gObjIsConnected(aIndex) == TRUE )
   413: 		{
   414: 			if ( strcmp(szMember, gObj[aIndex].Name) == 0 )
   415: 			{
   416: 				GCResultSend(aIndex, 0x51, lpMsg->Result);
   417: 				HereUserIndex = aIndex;
   418: 			}
   419: 		}
   420: 	}
   421: 
   422: 	if ( lpMsg->Result == 1 )
   423: 	{
   424: 		lpNode = gGuildClass.AddMember(szGuildName, szMember, HereUserIndex, -1, 0, lpMsg->pServer);
   425: 
   426: 		if ( HereUserIndex >= 0 && lpNode != NULL )
   427: 		{
   428: 			gObj[HereUserIndex].Guild = lpNode;
   429: 			gObj[HereUserIndex].GuildStatus = 0;
   430: 			gObj[HereUserIndex].GuildNumber = lpNode->Number;
   431: 			gObj[HereUserIndex].GuildUnionTimeStamp = 0;
   432: 			memcpy(gObj[HereUserIndex].GuildName,szGuildName,sizeof(gObj[HereUserIndex].GuildName));
   433: 			gViewport.GCViewportSimpleGuildSend(&gObj[HereUserIndex]);
   434: 		}
   435: 	}
   436: }

   438: void GDGuildMemberDel(int aIndex, LPSTR Name, LPSTR MemberId)
   439: {
   440: 	SDHP_GUILDMEMBERDEL pMsg;
   441: 
   442: 	pMsg.h.type = 0xC1;
   443: 	pMsg.h.head = 0x33;
   444: 	pMsg.h.size = sizeof(pMsg);
   445: 	pMsg.NumberH = SET_NUMBERHB(aIndex);
   446: 	pMsg.NumberL = SET_NUMBERLB(aIndex);
   447: 	memcpy(pMsg.MemberID, MemberId, sizeof(pMsg.MemberID));
   448: 	memcpy(pMsg.GuildName, Name, sizeof(pMsg.GuildName));
   449: 
   450: 	ESDataSend((BYTE*)&pMsg, pMsg.h.size);
   451: }
   452: 
   453: void GDGuildMemberDelResult(SDHP_GUILDMEMBERDEL_RESULT * lpMsg)
   454: {
   455: 	int aIndex = -1;
   456: 	char szMember[11];
   457: 	char szGuildName[9];
   458: 
   459: 	memcpy(szMember, lpMsg->MemberID, sizeof(szMember));
   460: 	memcpy(szGuildName, lpMsg->GuildName, sizeof(szGuildName));
   461: 
   462: 	if ( lpMsg->Flag == 1 )
   463: 	{
   464: 		aIndex = MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->NumberH, lpMsg->NumberL);
   465: 
   466: 		if ( gObjIsConnected(aIndex) == TRUE )
   467: 		{
   468: 			if ( strcmp(szMember, gObj[aIndex].Name) == 0 )
   469: 			{
   470: 				GCResultSend(aIndex, 0x53, lpMsg->Result);
   471: 			}
   472: 			else
   473: 			{
   474: 				GCResultSend(aIndex, 0x53, 5);
   475: 			}
   476: 		}
   477: 	}
   478: 
   479: 	if ( lpMsg->Result == 1 )
   480: 	{
   481: 		int iMember = 0;
   482: 
   483: 		while ( true )
   484: 		{
   485: 			if ( gObj[iMember].Connected > OBJECT_LOGGED)
   486: 			{
   487: 				if ( gObj[iMember].GuildNumber > 0 )
   488: 				{
   489: 					if ( gObj[iMember].Name[0] == szMember[0] && gObj[iMember].Name[1] == szMember[1] )
   490: 					{
   491: 						if ( strcmp(gObj[iMember].Name, szMember) == 0 )
   492: 						{
   493: 							GCGuildViewportDelNow(iMember, FALSE);
   494: 							gObj[iMember].Guild = NULL;
   495: 							gObj[iMember].GuildNumber = 0;
   496: 							gObj[iMember].GuildStatus = -1;
   497: 							gObj[iMember].GuildUnionTimeStamp = 0;
   498: 							memset(gObj[iMember].GuildName,0,sizeof(gObj[iMember].GuildName));
   499: 							gObjNotifyUpdateUnionV1(&gObj[iMember]);
   500: 							break;
   501: 						}
   502: 					}
   503: 				}
   504: 			}
   505: 
   506: 			if ( iMember < MAX_OBJECT-1 )
   507: 			{
   508: 				iMember++;
   509: 			}
   510: 			else
   511: 			{
   512: 				break;
   513: 			}
   514: 		}
   515: 
   516: 		gGuildClass.DelMember(szGuildName, szMember);
   517: 	}
   518: }

   536: 	PMSG_SIMPLE_GUILDVIEWPORT pMsg;

   544: 	int GuildUserOfs = sizeof(PMSG_SIMPLE_GUILDVIEWPORT_COUNT);
   545: 
   546: 	for ( int n=OBJECT_START_USER;n<MAX_OBJECT;n++)
   547: 	{

   551: 			{
   552: 				if ( strcmp(lpMsg->MemberID, gObj[n].Name ) == 0 )
   553: 				{
   554: 					#if(GAMESERVER_TYPE==1)
   555: 					szGuildName[8] = NULL;
   556: 					gCastleSiege.GetCsJoinSide(szGuildName,&gObj[n].CsJoinSide,&gObj[n].CsGuildInvolved);
   557: 					gCastleSiege.NotifySelfCsJoinSide(n);
   558: 					#endif
   559: 					memcpy(gObj[n].GuildName,szGuildName,sizeof(gObj[n].GuildName));
   560: 					gObj[n].Guild = gGuildClass.SearchGuild(gObj[n].GuildName);
   561: 
   562: 					if ( gObj[n].Guild != NULL )
   563: 					{
   564: 						gObj[n].GuildStatus = lpMsg->btGuildStatus;
   565: 						gObj[n].GuildNumber = gObj[n].Guild->Number;

   570: 							gNotice.GCNoticeSend(n,2,0,0,0,0,0,"%s",gObj[n].Guild->Notice);

   574: 						pMsg.NumberH = SET_NUMBERHB(n) & 0x7F;
   575: 						pMsg.NumberL = SET_NUMBERLB(n);
   576: 
   577: 						if ( strcmp(gObj[n].Guild->Names[0], gObj[n].Name) == 0 )
   578: 						{
   579: 							pMsg.NumberH |= 0x80;
   580: 						}
   581: 
   582: 						pMsg.btGuildStatus = gObj[n].GuildStatus;
   583: 
   584: 						if ( gObj[n].Guild != NULL )
   585: 						{
   586: 							pMsg.btGuildType = gObj[n].Guild->GuildType;
   587: 						}
   588: 						else

   590: 							pMsg.btGuildType = G_TYPE_NONE;
   591: 						}
   592: 
   593: 						//Season 4.5 Addon start
   594:                         if(gCastleSiegeSync.CheckCastleOwnerMember(gObj[n].Index) == 1 ||
   595:                            gCastleSiegeSync.CheckCastleOwnerUnionMember(gObj[n].Index) == 1)

   598:                         }

   602:                         }
   603:                         //Season 4.5 Addon end
```

```
   160: void GDCharClose(int type, char* GuildName, char* Name)
   161: {
   162: 	SDHP_USERCLOSE pMsg;
   163: 
   164: 	pMsg.h.type = 0xC1;
   165: 	pMsg.h.size = sizeof(pMsg);
   166: 	pMsg.h.head = 0x02;
   167: 	pMsg.Type = type;
   168: 	memcpy(pMsg.CharName, Name, sizeof(pMsg.CharName));
   169: 	memset(pMsg.GuildName, 0, sizeof(pMsg.GuildName));
   170: 
   171: 	if ( GuildName != NULL )
   172: 	{
   173: 		memcpy(pMsg.GuildName, GuildName, sizeof(pMsg.GuildName));
   174: 	}
   175: 
   176: 	ESDataSend((BYTE*)&pMsg, pMsg.h.size);
   177: }
   178: 
   179: void GDCharCloseRecv(SDHP_USERCLOSE * lpMsg )
   180: {
   181: 	char szName[11];
   182: 	char szGuildName[9];
   183: 
   184: 	memset(szName, 0, sizeof(szName));

   186: 	memcpy(szName, lpMsg->CharName, sizeof(szName));
   187: 	memcpy(szGuildName, lpMsg->GuildName, sizeof(szGuildName));
   188: 
   189: 	gGuildClass.CloseMember(szGuildName, szName);
   190: }

   192: void GDGuildCreateSend(int aIndex, char* Name, char* Master,BYTE* Mark, int iGuildType)
   193: {
   194: 	SDHP_GUILDCREATE pMsg;
   195: 
   196: 	pMsg.h.type = 0xC1;
   197: 	pMsg.h.head = 0x30;
   198: 	pMsg.h.size = sizeof(pMsg);
   199: 
   200: 	memcpy(pMsg.Mark, Mark, sizeof(pMsg.Mark));
   201: 	memcpy(pMsg.Master, Master, sizeof(pMsg.Master));
   202: 	memcpy(pMsg.GuildName, Name, sizeof(pMsg.GuildName));
   203: 	pMsg.NumberH = SET_NUMBERHB(aIndex);
   204: 	pMsg.NumberL = SET_NUMBERLB(aIndex);

   207: 	ESDataSend((BYTE*)&pMsg, pMsg.h.size);

   210: void GDGuildCreateRecv(SDHP_GUILDCREATE_RESULT * lpMsg)
   211: {
   212: 	int aIndex = -1;
   213: 	PMSG_GUILDCREATED_RESULT pMsg;
   214: 	GUILD_INFO_STRUCT * lpNode;
   215: 	char szMaster[11];
   216: 	char szGuildName[9];
   217: 
   218: 	memcpy(szMaster, lpMsg->Master, sizeof(szMaster));
   219: 	memcpy(szGuildName, lpMsg->GuildName, sizeof(szGuildName));
   220: 
   221: 	if (lpMsg->Result == 0 )
   222: 	{
   223: 		aIndex = MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->NumberH, lpMsg->NumberL);
   224: 
   225: 		PMSG_GUILDCREATED_RESULT pResult;
   226: 
   227: 		pResult.h.set(0x56, sizeof(pResult));
   228: 		pResult.Result = G_ERROR_NONE;
   229: 		pResult.btGuildType = lpMsg->btGuildType;
   230: 
   231: 		DataSend(aIndex, (LPBYTE)&pResult, pResult.h.size);
   232: 
   233: 		if ( gObj[aIndex].Interface.use != 0 && gObj[aIndex].Interface.type == 5 )
   234: 		{
   235: 			gObj[aIndex].Interface.use = 0;
   236: 		}
   237: 		return;
   238: 	}
   239: 
   240: 	if ( lpMsg->Flag == 1 )
   241: 	{
   242: 		aIndex = MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->NumberH, lpMsg->NumberL);
   243: 
   244: 		if ( gObjIsConnected(aIndex) == TRUE )
   245: 		{
   246: 			if ( szMaster[0] == gObj[aIndex].Name[0] )
   247: 			{
   248: 				if ( strcmp(szMaster, gObj[aIndex].Name) == 0 )
   249: 				{
   250: 					pMsg.h.type = 0xC1;
   251: 					pMsg.h.head = 0x56;
   252: 					pMsg.h.size = sizeof(pMsg);
   253: 					pMsg.Result = lpMsg->Result;
   254: 					pMsg.btGuildType = lpMsg->btGuildType;
   255: 
   256: 					DataSend(aIndex, (LPBYTE)&pMsg, pMsg.h.size);
   257: 				}
   258: 			}
   259: 		}
   260: 	}
   261: 
   262: 	if ( lpMsg->Result == 1 )
   263: 	{
   264: 		lpNode = gGuildClass.AddGuild(lpMsg->GuildNumber, szGuildName, lpMsg->Mark, szMaster, 0);
   265: 
   266: 		if ( aIndex >= 0 && lpNode != NULL )
   267: 		{
   268: 			gObj[aIndex].Guild = lpNode;
   269: 			gObj[aIndex].GuildNumber = lpMsg->GuildNumber;
   270: 			gObj[aIndex].GuildStatus = G_MASTER;
   271: 			lpNode->GuildRival = 0;
   272: 			lpNode->GuildUnion = 0;
   273: 			lpNode->TimeStamp = 0;
   274: 			gObj[aIndex].GuildUnionTimeStamp = 0;
   275: 			memcpy(gObj[aIndex].GuildName,szGuildName,sizeof(gObj[aIndex].GuildName));
   276: 			gViewport.GCViewportSimpleGuildSend(&gObj[aIndex]);
   277: 		}
   278: 	}
   279: }
   280: 
   281: void GDGuildDestroySend(int aIndex, LPSTR Name, LPSTR Master)
   282: {
   283: 	SDHP_GUILDDESTROY pMsg;
   284: 
   285: 	pMsg.h.type = 0xC1;
   286: 	pMsg.h.head = 0x31;
   287: 	pMsg.h.size = sizeof(pMsg);
   288: 	pMsg.NumberH = SET_NUMBERHB(aIndex);
   289: 	pMsg.NumberL = SET_NUMBERLB(aIndex);
   290: 	memcpy(pMsg.Master, Master, sizeof(pMsg.Master));
   291: 	memcpy(pMsg.GuildName, Name, sizeof(pMsg.GuildName));
```

```
   180: {
   181: 	char szName[11];
   182: 	char szGuildName[9];
   183: 
   184: 	memset(szName, 0, sizeof(szName));
   185: 	memset(szGuildName, 0, sizeof(szGuildName));
   186: 	memcpy(szName, lpMsg->CharName, sizeof(szName));
   187: 	memcpy(szGuildName, lpMsg->GuildName, sizeof(szGuildName));
   188: 
   189: 	gGuildClass.CloseMember(szGuildName, szName);
   190: }
   191: 
   192: void GDGuildCreateSend(int aIndex, char* Name, char* Master,BYTE* Mark, int iGuildType)
   193: {
   194: 	SDHP_GUILDCREATE pMsg;
   195: 
   196: 	pMsg.h.type = 0xC1;
   197: 	pMsg.h.head = 0x30;
   198: 	pMsg.h.size = sizeof(pMsg);
   199: 
   200: 	memcpy(pMsg.Mark, Mark, sizeof(pMsg.Mark));
   201: 	memcpy(pMsg.Master, Master, sizeof(pMsg.Master));
   202: 	memcpy(pMsg.GuildName, Name, sizeof(pMsg.GuildName));
   203: 	pMsg.NumberH = SET_NUMBERHB(aIndex);
   204: 	pMsg.NumberL = SET_NUMBERLB(aIndex);
   205: 	pMsg.btGuildType = iGuildType;
   206: 
   207: 	ESDataSend((BYTE*)&pMsg, pMsg.h.size);
   208: }
   209: 
   210: void GDGuildCreateRecv(SDHP_GUILDCREATE_RESULT * lpMsg)
   211: {
   212: 	int aIndex = -1;
   213: 	PMSG_GUILDCREATED_RESULT pMsg;
   214: 	GUILD_INFO_STRUCT * lpNode;
   215: 	char szMaster[11];
   216: 	char szGuildName[9];
   217: 
   218: 	memcpy(szMaster, lpMsg->Master, sizeof(szMaster));
   219: 	memcpy(szGuildName, lpMsg->GuildName, sizeof(szGuildName));
   220: 
   221: 	if (lpMsg->Result == 0 )
   222: 	{
   223: 		aIndex = MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->NumberH, lpMsg->NumberL);
   224: 
   225: 		PMSG_GUILDCREATED_RESULT pResult;
   226: 
   227: 		pResult.h.set(0x56, sizeof(pResult));
   228: 		pResult.Result = G_ERROR_NONE;
   229: 		pResult.btGuildType = lpMsg->btGuildType;
   230: 
   231: 		DataSend(aIndex, (LPBYTE)&pResult, pResult.h.size);
   232: 
   233: 		if ( gObj[aIndex].Interface.use != 0 && gObj[aIndex].Interface.type == 5 )
   234: 		{
   235: 			gObj[aIndex].Interface.use = 0;
   236: 		}
   237: 		return;
   238: 	}
   239: 
   240: 	if ( lpMsg->Flag == 1 )
   241: 	{
   242: 		aIndex = MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->NumberH, lpMsg->NumberL);
   243: 
   244: 		if ( gObjIsConnected(aIndex) == TRUE )
   245: 		{
   246: 			if ( szMaster[0] == gObj[aIndex].Name[0] )
   247: 			{
   248: 				if ( strcmp(szMaster, gObj[aIndex].Name) == 0 )
   249: 				{
   250: 					pMsg.h.type = 0xC1;
   251: 					pMsg.h.head = 0x56;
   252: 					pMsg.h.size = sizeof(pMsg);
   253: 					pMsg.Result = lpMsg->Result;
   254: 					pMsg.btGuildType = lpMsg->btGuildType;
   255: 
   256: 					DataSend(aIndex, (LPBYTE)&pMsg, pMsg.h.size);
   257: 				}
   258: 			}
   259: 		}
   260: 	}
   261: 
   262: 	if ( lpMsg->Result == 1 )
   263: 	{
   264: 		lpNode = gGuildClass.AddGuild(lpMsg->GuildNumber, szGuildName, lpMsg->Mark, szMaster, 0);
   265: 
   266: 		if ( aIndex >= 0 && lpNode != NULL )
   267: 		{
   268: 			gObj[aIndex].Guild = lpNode;
   269: 			gObj[aIndex].GuildNumber = lpMsg->GuildNumber;
   270: 			gObj[aIndex].GuildStatus = G_MASTER;
   271: 			lpNode->GuildRival = 0;
   272: 			lpNode->GuildUnion = 0;
   273: 			lpNode->TimeStamp = 0;
   274: 			gObj[aIndex].GuildUnionTimeStamp = 0;
   275: 			memcpy(gObj[aIndex].GuildName,szGuildName,sizeof(gObj[aIndex].GuildName));
   276: 			gViewport.GCViewportSimpleGuildSend(&gObj[aIndex]);
   277: 		}
   278: 	}
   279: }
   280: 
   281: void GDGuildDestroySend(int aIndex, LPSTR Name, LPSTR Master)
   282: {
   283: 	SDHP_GUILDDESTROY pMsg;
   284: 
   285: 	pMsg.h.type = 0xC1;
   286: 	pMsg.h.head = 0x31;
   287: 	pMsg.h.size = sizeof(pMsg);
   288: 	pMsg.NumberH = SET_NUMBERHB(aIndex);
   289: 	pMsg.NumberL = SET_NUMBERLB(aIndex);
   290: 	memcpy(pMsg.Master, Master, sizeof(pMsg.Master));
   291: 	memcpy(pMsg.GuildName, Name, sizeof(pMsg.GuildName));
   292: 
   293: 	ESDataSend((BYTE*)&pMsg, pMsg.h.size);
   294: }
   295: 
   296: void GDGuildDestroyRecv(SDHP_GUILDDESTROY_RESULT * lpMsg)
   297: {
   298: 	int aIndex = -1;
   299: 	char szMaster[11];
   300: 	char szGuildName[9];
   301: 
   302: 	memcpy(szMaster, lpMsg->Master, sizeof(szMaster));
   303: 	memcpy(szGuildName, lpMsg->GuildName, sizeof(szGuildName));
   304: 
   305: 	if ( lpMsg->Flag == 1 )
   306: 	{
   307: 		aIndex = MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->NumberH, lpMsg->NumberL);
   308: 
   309: 		if ( gObjIsConnected(aIndex) == TRUE )
   310: 		{
   311: 			if ( szMaster[0] == gObj[aIndex].Name[0] )
   312: 			{
   313: 				if ( strcmp(szMaster, gObj[aIndex].Name) == 0 )
   314: 				{
   315: 					GCResultSend(aIndex, 0x53, lpMsg->Result);
   316: 
   317: 					if ( lpMsg->Result == 1 || lpMsg->Result == 4 )
   318: 					{
   319: 						GCGuildViewportDelNow(aIndex, TRUE);
   320: 					}
   321: 				}
   322: 			}
   323: 		}
   324: 	}
   325: 
   326: 	if ( lpMsg->Result == 1 || lpMsg->Result == 4)
   327: 	{
   328: 		GUILD_INFO_STRUCT * lpGuild = gGuildClass.SearchGuild(szGuildName);
   329: 
   330: 		if ( lpGuild == NULL )
   331: 		{
   332: 			return;
   333: 		}
   334: 
   335: 		GUILD_INFO_STRUCT * lpRival = gGuildClass.SearchGuild_Number(lpGuild->GuildRival);
```

```
   752: 	{
   753: 		return;
   754: 	}
   755:

   757:

   765:

   770: 
   771: 	this->GCEventInventoryEnableSend(lpObj->Index);

   773: 	#endif
```

```
   285: bool CEventInventory::EventInventoryAddItemStack(LPOBJ lpObj,int SourceSlot,int TargetSlot) // OK
   286: {
   287: 	#if(GAMESERVER_UPDATE>=802)
   288: 
   289: 	int MaxStack,CreateItemIndex;
   290: 
   291: 	if(lpObj->EventInventory[SourceSlot].IsItem() == 0 || lpObj->EventInventory[TargetSlot].IsItem() == 0)
   292: 	{
   293: 		return 0;
   294: 	}
   295: 
   296: 	if(lpObj->EventInventory[SourceSlot].m_Index != lpObj->EventInventory[TargetSlot].m_Index || lpObj->EventInventory[SourceSlot].m_Level != lpObj->EventInventory[TargetSlot].m_Level || lpObj->EventInventory[SourceSlot].m_SocketOptionBonus != lpObj->EventInventory[TargetSlot].m_SocketOptionBonus)
   297: 	{
   298: 		return 0;
   299: 	}
   300: 
   301: 	if((MaxStack=gItemStack.GetItemMaxStack(lpObj->EventInventory[SourceSlot].m_Index)) <= 0)
   302: 	{
   303: 		return 0;
   304: 	}
   305: 
   306: 	if(lpObj->EventInventory[TargetSlot].m_Durability >= MaxStack)
   307: 	{
   308: 		return 0;
   309: 	}
   310: 
   311: 	int AddDur = (int)(MaxStack-lpObj->EventInventory[TargetSlot].m_Durability);
   312: 
   313: 	AddDur = ((AddDur>lpObj->EventInventory[SourceSlot].m_Durability)?(int)lpObj->EventInventory[SourceSlot].m_Durability:AddDur);
   314: 
   315: 	lpObj->EventInventory[SourceSlot].m_Durability -= AddDur;
   316: 
   317: 	lpObj->EventInventory[TargetSlot].m_Durability += AddDur;
   318: 
   319: 	if(lpObj->EventInventory[TargetSlot].m_Durability == MaxStack && (CreateItemIndex=gItemStack.GetCreateItemIndex(lpObj->EventInventory[SourceSlot].m_Index)) != -1)
   320: 	{
   321: 		this->EventInventoryDelItem(lpObj->Index,TargetSlot);
   322: 		this->GCEventItemDeleteSend(lpObj->Index,TargetSlot,1);
   323: 		GDCreateItemSend(lpObj->Index,0xEB,0,0,CreateItemIndex,(BYTE)lpObj->EventInventory[SourceSlot].m_Level,0,0,0,0,lpObj->Index,0,0,0,0,0,lpObj->EventInventory[SourceSlot].m_SocketOptionBonus,0);
   324: 	}
   325: 	else
   326: 	{
   327: 		this->GCEventItemDurSend(lpObj->Index,TargetSlot,(BYTE)lpObj->EventInventory[TargetSlot].m_Durability);
   328: 	}
   329: 
   330: 	if(lpObj->EventInventory[SourceSlot].m_Durability < 1)
   331: 	{
   332: 		this->EventInventoryDelItem(lpObj->Index,SourceSlot);
   333: 		this->GCEventItemDeleteSend(lpObj->Index,SourceSlot,1);
   334: 	}
   335: 
   336: 	return 1;
   337: 
   338: 	#else
   339: 
   340: 	return 0;
   341: 
   342: 	#endif
```

```
    33: 		{
    34: 			continue;

    36:

    43: 				if(lpObj->Effect[i].m_index == EFFECT_HAWK_FIGURINE || lpObj->Effect[i].m_index == EFFECT_GOAT_FIGURINE || lpObj->Effect[i].m_index == EFFECT_OAK_CHARM || lpObj->Effect[i].m_index == EFFECT_MAPLE_CHARM || lpObj->Effect[i].m_index == EFFECT_GOLDEN_OAK_CHARM || lpObj->Effect[i].m_index == EFFECT_GOLDEN_MAPLE_CHARM || lpObj->Effect[i].m_index == EFFECT_WORN_HORSESHOE)
    44: 				{
    45: 					EFFECT_INFO* lpInfo = gEffectManager.GetInfo(lpObj->Effect[i].m_index);

    49: 						if(this->CheckInventoryEquipment(lpObj,lpInfo->ItemIndex) == 0)
    50: 						{

    52: 						}
    53: 					}
    54: 				}
    55: 			}
    56: 		}
    57: 	}
    58:

    99: 				if(lpObj->Inventory[n].m_Durability == 254)
```

```
   203: int CItemBag::GetItemLevel(ITEM_BAG_INFO* lpInfo) // OK
   204: {
   205: 	if(lpInfo->MinLevel >= lpInfo->MaxLevel)
   206: 	{
   207: 		return lpInfo->MinLevel;
   208: 	}
   209: 
   210: 	return (lpInfo->MinLevel+(GetLargeRand()%((lpInfo->MaxLevel-lpInfo->MinLevel)+1)));
   211: }
   212: 
   213: int CItemBag::GetItemNewOption(ITEM_BAG_INFO* lpInfo) // OK
   214: {
   215: 	int count = 0;
   216: 
   217: 	int NewOption = 0;
   218: 
   219: 	while(true)
   220: 	{
   221: 		if(count >= lpInfo->NewOption || count >= MAX_EXC_OPTION)
   222: 		{
   223: 			break;
   224: 		}
   225: 
   226: 		//int value = 1 << (GetLargeRand()%MAX_EXC_OPTION);
   227: 
   228: 		BYTE ExcOption;
   229: 
   230: 		if(gItemExOptionRate.GetExcellentOptionByRate(lpInfo->Index,NewOption,&ExcOption) == 0)
   231: 		{
   232: 			break;
   233: 		}
   234: 
   235: 		if((NewOption & ExcOption) == 0)
   236: 		{
   237: 			NewOption |= ExcOption;
   238: 			count++;
   239: 		}
   240: 	}
   241: 
   242: 	return NewOption;
   243: }
   244: 
   245: int CItemBag::GetItemSetOption(ITEM_BAG_INFO* lpInfo) // OK
   246: {
   247: 	if(lpInfo->SetOption == 0)
   248: 	{
   249: 		return 0;
   250: 	}
   251: 
   252: 	int SetOption = 0;
   253: 
   254: 	for(int n=0;n < MAX_SET_ITEM_OPTION_INDEX;n++)
   255: 	{
   256: 		if(gSetItemType.GetSetItemOptionIndex(lpInfo->Index,n) <= 0)
   257: 		{
   258: 			continue;
   259: 		}
   260: 
   261: 		SetOption = ((SetOption==0)?(n+1):(((GetLargeRand()%2)==0)?(n+1):SetOption));
   262: 	}
   263: 
   264: 	if(SetOption == 0)
   265: 	{
   266: 		return 0;
   267: 	}
   268: 
   269: 	if((GetLargeRand()%100) < 80)
   270: 	{
   271: 		SetOption |= 4;
   272: 	}
   273: 	else
   274: 	{
   275: 		SetOption |= 8;
   276: 	}
   277: 
   278: 	return SetOption;
   279: }

   287: 	ty = ((ty<1)?1:ty);

   291: 		(*ox) = ((GetLargeRand()%(tx+1))*((GetLargeRand()%2==0)?-1:1))+x;

   296: 			return 1;
   297: 		}
   298: 	}
   299: 
   300: 	return 0;
   301: }
```

```
   115: 			{
   116: 				continue;
   117: 			}
   118: 
   119: 			if(it->ItemMaxIndex != -1 && it->ItemMaxIndex < lpItem->m_Index)
   120: 			{
   121: 				continue;
   122: 			}
   123: 
   124: 			if(it->ItemOption1 != -1 && it->ItemOption1 > lpItem->m_Option1)
   125: 			{
   126: 				continue;
   127: 			}
   128: 
   129: 			if(it->ItemOption2 != -1 && it->ItemOption2 > lpItem->m_Option2)
   130: 			{
   131: 				continue;
   132: 			}
   133: 
   134: 			if(it->ItemOption3 != -1 && it->ItemOption3 > lpItem->m_Option3)
   135: 			{
   136: 				continue;
   137: 			}
   138: 
   139: 			if(it->ItemNewOption != -1 && (lpItem->m_NewOption & it->ItemNewOption) == 0)
   140: 			{
   141: 				continue;
   142: 			}
   143: 
   144: 			result = 1;
   145: 
   146: 			lpItem->m_SpecialIndex[index] = it->OptionIndex;
   147: 
   148: 			lpItem->m_SpecialValue[index] = it->OptionValue;
   149: 		}
   150: 	}
   151: 
   152: 	return result;
   153: }

   155: void CItemOption::CalcItemCommonOption(LPOBJ lpObj,bool flag) // OK
   156: {
   157: 	for(int n=0;n < INVENTORY_WEAR_SIZE;n++)
   158: 	{
   159: 		if(lpObj->Inventory[n].IsItem() != 0 && lpObj->Inventory[n].m_IsValidItem != 0 && lpObj->Inventory[n].m_Durability != 0)
   160: 		{
   161: 			this->InsertOption(lpObj,&lpObj->Inventory[n],flag);
   162: 		}
   163: 	}
   164: }
   165: 
   166: void CItemOption::InsertOption(LPOBJ lpObj,CItem* lpItem,bool flag) // OK
   167: {
   168: 	for(int n=0;n < MAX_SPECIAL;n++)
   169: 	{
   170: 		int index = lpItem->m_SpecialIndex[n];
   171: 
   172: 		int value = lpItem->m_SpecialValue[n];
   173: 
   174: 		if(flag == 0)
   175: 		{
   176: 			if(index == ITEM_OPTION_ADD_WING_LEADERSHIP)
   177: 			{
   178: 				continue;
   179: 			}
   180: 		}
   181: 		else
   182: 		{
   183: 			if(index != ITEM_OPTION_ADD_WING_LEADERSHIP)
   184: 			{
   185: 				continue;
   186: 			}
   187: 		}
   188: 
   189: 		switch(index)
   190: 		{
   191: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_PHYSI_DAMAGE:
   192: 				lpObj->PhysiDamageMinRight += value;
   193: 				lpObj->PhysiDamageMaxRight += value;
   194: 				lpObj->PhysiDamageMinLeft += value;
   195: 				lpObj->PhysiDamageMaxLeft += value;
   196: 				break;
   197: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_MAGIC_DAMAGE:
   198: 				lpObj->MagicDamageMin += value;
   199: 				lpObj->MagicDamageMax += value;
   200: 				break;
   201: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_DEFENSE_SUCCESS_RATE:
   202: 				lpObj->DefenseSuccessRate += value;
   203: 				break;
   204: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_DEFENSE:
   205: 				lpObj->Defense += value;
   206: 				break;
   207: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_CRITICAL_DAMAGE_RATE:
   208: 				lpObj->CriticalDamageRate += value;
   209: 				break;
   210: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_HP_RECOVERY_RATE:
   211: 				lpObj->HPRecoveryRate += value;
   212: 				break;
   213: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_MONEY_AMOUNT_DROP_RATE:
   214: 				lpObj->MoneyAmountDropRate += value;
   215: 				break;
   216: 			case ITEM_OPTION_MUL_DEFENSE_SUCCESS_RATE:
   217: 				lpObj->DefenseSuccessRate += (lpObj->DefenseSuccessRate*value)/100;
   218: 				break;
   219: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_DAMAGE_REFLECT:
   220: 				lpObj->DamageReflect += value;
   221: 				break;
   222: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_DAMAGE_REDUCTION:
   223: 				lpObj->DamageReduction[DAMAGE_REDUCTION_EXCELLENT_ITEM] += value;
   224: 				break;
   225: 			case ITEM_OPTION_MUL_MP:
   226: 				lpObj->AddMana += (int)((lpObj->MaxMana*value)/100);
   227: 				break;
   228: 			case ITEM_OPTION_MUL_HP:
   229: 				lpObj->AddLife += (int)((lpObj->MaxLife*value)/100);
   230: 				break;
   231: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_EXCELLENT_DAMAGE_RATE:
   232: 				lpObj->ExcellentDamageRate += value;
   233: 				break;
   234: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_PHYSI_DAMAGE_BY_LEVEL:
   235: 				lpObj->PhysiDamageMinRight += ((lpObj->Level+lpObj->MasterLevel)/value);
   236: 				lpObj->PhysiDamageMaxRight += ((lpObj->Level+lpObj->MasterLevel)/value);
   237: 				lpObj->PhysiDamageMinLeft += ((lpObj->Level+lpObj->MasterLevel)/value);
   238: 				lpObj->PhysiDamageMaxLeft += ((lpObj->Level+lpObj->MasterLevel)/value);
   239: 				break;
   240: 			case ITEM_OPTION_MUL_PHYSI_DAMAGE:
   241: 				lpObj->PhysiDamageMinRight += (lpObj->PhysiDamageMinRight*value)/100;
   242: 				lpObj->PhysiDamageMaxRight += (lpObj->PhysiDamageMaxRight*value)/100;
   243: 				lpObj->PhysiDamageMinLeft += (lpObj->PhysiDamageMinLeft*value)/100;
   244: 				lpObj->PhysiDamageMaxLeft += (lpObj->PhysiDamageMaxLeft*value)/100;
   245: 				break;
   246: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_MAGIC_DAMAGE_BY_LEVEL:
   247: 				lpObj->MagicDamageMin += ((lpObj->Level+lpObj->MasterLevel)/value);
   248: 				lpObj->MagicDamageMax += ((lpObj->Level+lpObj->MasterLevel)/value);
   249: 				break;
   250: 			case ITEM_OPTION_MUL_MAGIC_DAMAGE:
   251: 				lpObj->MagicDamageMin += (lpObj->MagicDamageMin*value)/100;
   252: 				lpObj->MagicDamageMax += (lpObj->MagicDamageMax*value)/100;
   253: 				break;
   254: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_SPEED:
   255: 				lpObj->PhysiSpeed += value;
   256: 				lpObj->MagicSpeed += value;
   257: 				break;
   258: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_HUNT_HP:
   259: 				lpObj->HuntHP += value;
   260: 				break;
   261: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_HUNT_MP:
   262: 				lpObj->HuntMP += value;
   263: 				break;
   264: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_WING_HP:
   265: 				lpObj->AddLife += 50+(value*lpItem->m_Level);

   267: 			case ITEM_OPTION_ADD_WING_MP:
```

```
   143: 						TargetCoin3 += (int)(it->Coin3*lpTarget->Trade[n].m_Durability);
   144: 					}
   145: 				}
   146: 			}
   147: 		}
   148: 	}
   149: 
   150: 	if(lpObj->Money < (DWORD)Money) //OK
   151: 	{
   152: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpObj->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(844),Money);
   153: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpTarget->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(848),lpObj->Name);
   154: 		return 0;
   155: 	}
   156: 
   157: 	if (lpObj->Coin1 < Coin1) //OK
   158: 	{
   159: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpObj->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(845),Coin1);
   160: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpTarget->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(848),lpObj->Name);
   161: 		return 0;
   162: 	}
   163: 
   164: 	if (lpObj->Coin2 < Coin2) //OK
   165: 	{
   166: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpObj->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(846),Coin2);
   167: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpTarget->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(848),lpObj->Name);
   168: 		return 0;
   169: 	}
   170: 
   171: 	if (lpObj->Coin3 < Coin3) //OK
   172: 	{
   173: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpObj->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(847),Coin3);
   174: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpTarget->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(848),lpObj->Name);
   175: 		return 0;
   176: 	}
   177: 
   178: 	if(lpTarget->Money < (DWORD)TargetMoney) //OK
   179: 	{
   180: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpTarget->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(844),TargetMoney);
   181: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpObj->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(848),lpTarget->Name);
   182: 		return 0;
   183: 	}
   184: 
   185: 	if (lpTarget->Coin1 < TargetCoin1) //OK
   186: 	{
   187: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpTarget->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(845),TargetCoin1);
   188: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpObj->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(848),lpTarget->Name);
   189: 		return 0;
   190: 	}
   191: 
   192: 	if (lpTarget->Coin2 < TargetCoin2) //OK
   193: 	{
   194: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpTarget->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(846),TargetCoin2);
   195: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpObj->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(848),lpTarget->Name);
   196: 		return 0;
   197: 	}
   198: 
   199: 	if (lpTarget->Coin3 < TargetCoin3) //OK
   200: 	{
   201: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpTarget->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(847),TargetCoin3);
   202: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpObj->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(848),lpTarget->Name);
   203: 		return 0;
   204: 	}
   205: 
   206: 	if (Coin1 > 0 || Coin2 > 0 || Coin3 > 0)
   207: 	{
   208: 		GDSetCoinSend(lpObj->Index, -(Coin1), -(Coin2), -(Coin3),"TradeValue");
   209: 	}
   210: 
   211: 	if (TargetCoin1 > 0 || TargetCoin2 > 0 || TargetCoin3 > 0)
   212: 	{
   213: 		GDSetCoinSend(lpTarget->Index, -(TargetCoin1), -(TargetCoin2), -(TargetCoin3),"TradeValue");
   214: 	}
   215: 
   216: 	lpObj->Money -= Money;
   217: 	GCMoneySend(lpObj->Index,lpObj->Money);
   218: 
   219: 	lpTarget->Money -= TargetMoney;
   220: 	GCMoneySend(lpTarget->Index,lpTarget->Money);
   221: 
   222: 	return 1;
   223: }
```

```
   115: void JGMapServerMoveRecv(SDHP_MAP_SERVER_MOVE_RECV* lpMsg) // OK
   116: {
   117: 	if(gObjIsAccountValid(lpMsg->index,lpMsg->account) == 0)
   118: 	{
   119: 		LogAdd(LOG_RED,"[JGMapServerMoveRecv] Invalid Account [%d](%s)",lpMsg->index,lpMsg->account);
   120: 		CloseClient(lpMsg->index);
   121: 		return;
   122: 	}
   123: 
   124: 
   125: 	if(lpMsg->result == 0 && gObj[lpMsg->index].MapMoveDisable != 1)
   126: 	{
   127: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpMsg->index,0,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(259));
   128: 		GJMapServerMoveSend(lpMsg->index,gServerInfo.m_ServerCode,0,125,125);
   129: 		return;
   130: 	}
   131: 
   132: 	PMSG_MAP_SERVER_MOVE_SEND pMsg = {0};
   133: 
   134: 	pMsg.header.set(0xB1,0x00,sizeof(pMsg));
   135: 
   136: 	if(lpMsg->result == 0)
   137: 	{
   138: 		gObj[lpMsg->index].State = OBJECT_PLAYING;
   139: 		DataSend(lpMsg->index,(BYTE*)&pMsg,pMsg.header.size);
   140: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpMsg->index,0,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(259));
   141: 		return;
   142: 	}
   143: 
   144: 	if(gMapServerManager.GetMapServerData(lpMsg->NextServerCode,pMsg.IpAddress,&pMsg.ServerPort) == 0)
   145: 	{
   146: 		gObj[lpMsg->index].State = OBJECT_PLAYING;
   147: 		GJMapServerMoveCancelSend(lpMsg->index);
   148: 		DataSend(lpMsg->index,(BYTE*)&pMsg,pMsg.header.size);
   149: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpMsg->index,0,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(259));
   150: 
   151: 		//gObjMoveGate(lpMsg->index,17);
   152: 		return;
   153: 	}
   154: 
   155: 	pMsg.ServerCode = gMapServerManager.GetMapServerGroup();
   156: 
   157: 	pMsg.AuthCode1 = lpMsg->AuthCode1;
   158: 
   159: 	pMsg.AuthCode2 = lpMsg->AuthCode2;
   160: 
   161: 	pMsg.AuthCode3 = lpMsg->AuthCode3;
   162: 
   163: 	pMsg.AuthCode4 = lpMsg->AuthCode4;
   164: 
   165: 	DataSend(lpMsg->index,(BYTE*)&pMsg,pMsg.header.size);
   166: 
   167: 	gObjectManager.CharacterGameClose(lpMsg->index);
   168: 
   169: 	gObj[lpMsg->index].MapServerMoveQuit = 1;
   170: 
   171: 	gObj[lpMsg->index].MapServerMoveQuitTickCount = GetTickCount();
   172: }

   174: void JGMapServerMoveAuthRecv(SDHP_MAP_SERVER_MOVE_AUTH_RECV* lpMsg) // OK
   175: {
   176: 	if(OBJECT_RANGE(lpMsg->index) == 0)
   177: 	{
   178: 		return;
   179: 	}
   180: 
   181: 	if((gObj[lpMsg->index].LoginMessageSend--) == 0)
   182: 	{
   183: 		CloseClient(lpMsg->index);
   184: 		return;
   185: 	}
   186: 
   187: 	if(gObj[lpMsg->index].MapServerMoveRequest == 0)
   188: 	{
   189: 		CloseClient(lpMsg->index);
   190: 		return;
   191: 	}
   192: 
   193: 	if(gObj[lpMsg->index].Connected != OBJECT_CONNECTED)
   194: 	{
   195: 		GJDisconnectAccountSend(lpMsg->index,lpMsg->account,gObj[lpMsg->index].IpAddr);
   196: 		return;
   197: 	}
   198: 
   199: 	if(lpMsg->result != 1)
   200: 	{
   201: 		GCMapServerMoveAuthSend(lpMsg->index,((gObj[lpMsg->index].LoginMessageCount>=3)?8:lpMsg->result));
   202: 		return;
   203: 	}
   204: 
   205: 	if(lpMsg->BlockCode != 0)
   206: 	{
   207: 		GJDisconnectAccountSend(lpMsg->index,lpMsg->account,gObj[lpMsg->index].IpAddr);
   208: 		GCMapServerMoveAuthSend(lpMsg->index,5);
   209: 		return;
   210: 	}
   211: 
   212: 	if(gServerInfo.m_ServerLock > lpMsg->AccountLevel)
   213: 	{
   214: 		GJDisconnectAccountSend(lpMsg->index,lpMsg->account,gObj[lpMsg->index].IpAddr);
   215: 		GCMapServerMoveAuthSend(lpMsg->index,9);
   216: 		return;
   217: 	}
   218: 
   219: 	gObj[lpMsg->index].Connected = OBJECT_LOGGED;
   220: 
   221: 	memcpy(gObj[lpMsg->index].Account,lpMsg->account,sizeof(gObj[lpMsg->index].Account));
   222: 
   223: 	memcpy(gObj[lpMsg->index].PersonalCode,lpMsg->PersonalCode,sizeof(gObj[lpMsg->index].PersonalCode));
   224: 
   225: 	gObj[lpMsg->index].AccountLevel = lpMsg->AccountLevel;
   226: 
   227: 	gObj[lpMsg->index].Lock = lpMsg->Lock;
   228: 
   229: 	memcpy(gObj[lpMsg->index].AccountExpireDate,lpMsg->AccountExpireDate,sizeof(gObj[lpMsg->index].AccountExpireDate));
   230: 
   231: 	GCMapServerMoveAuthSend(lpMsg->index,1);
   232: 
   233: 	gObj[lpMsg->index].EnableDelCharacter = 0;
   234: 
   235: 	gObj[lpMsg->index].LastServerCode = lpMsg->LastServerCode;
   236: 
   237: 	gObj[lpMsg->index].DestMap  = lpMsg->map;
   238: 
   239: 	gObj[lpMsg->index].DestX = lpMsg->x;
   240: 
   241: 	gObj[lpMsg->index].DestY = lpMsg->y;
   242: 
   243: 	GDCharacterInfoSend(lpMsg->index,lpMsg->name);
   244: 
   245: 	LogAddConnect(LOG_GREEN,"[Obj][%d] AddAccount (%s)",lpMsg->index,gObj[lpMsg->index].Account);
   246: }

   301: {

   303: 	{

   310: 	{

   312: 	}
   313: 
   314: 	gCustomAttack.OnAttackAlreadyConnected(&gObj[lpMsg->index]);
   315: 
   316: 	gCustomStore.OnPShopAlreadyConnected(&gObj[lpMsg->index]);
   317: 
   318: }

   320: void GJServerInfoSend() // OK
   321: {
   322: 	SDHP_JOIN_SERVER_INFO_SEND pMsg;
   323: 
   324: 	pMsg.header.set(0x00,sizeof(pMsg));
   325: 
   326: 	pMsg.type = 1;
   327: 
   328: 	pMsg.ServerPort = (WORD)gServerInfo.m_ServerPort;
   329: 
   330: 	strcpy_s(pMsg.ServerName,gServerInfo.m_ServerName);
   331: 
   332: 	pMsg.ServerCode = (WORD)gServerInfo.m_ServerCode;
   333: 
   334: 	gJoinServerConnection.DataSend((BYTE*)&pMsg,pMsg.header.size);
   335: }
```

```
   391: 		LogAdd(LOG_RED,"[DGLuckyItemRecv] Invalid Account [%d](%s)",lpMsg->index,lpMsg->account);
   392: 		CloseClient(lpMsg->index);
   393: 		return;
   394: 	}
   395: 
   396: 	LPOBJ lpObj = &gObj[lpMsg->index];
   397: 
   398: 	for(int n=0;n < lpMsg->count;n++)
   399: 	{
   400: 		SDHP_LUCKY_ITEM1* lpInfo = (SDHP_LUCKY_ITEM1*)(((BYTE*)lpMsg)+sizeof(SDHP_LUCKY_ITEM_RECV)+(sizeof(SDHP_LUCKY_ITEM1)*n));
   401: 
   402: 		if(INVENTORY_RANGE(lpInfo->slot) == 0)
   403: 		{
   404: 			continue;
   405: 		}
   406: 
   407: 		if(lpObj->Inventory[lpInfo->slot].IsItem() == 0)
   408: 		{
   409: 			continue;
   410: 		}
   411: 
   412: 		if(lpObj->Inventory[lpInfo->slot].IsLuckyItem() == 0)
   413: 		{
   414: 			continue;
   415: 		}
   416: 
   417: 		if(lpObj->Inventory[lpInfo->slot].m_Serial != lpInfo->serial)
   418: 		{
   419: 			continue;
   420: 		}
   421: 
   422: 		lpObj->Inventory[lpInfo->slot].m_DurabilitySmall = (WORD)lpInfo->DurabilitySmall;
   423: 	}
   424: 
   425: 	#endif
   426: }

   428: void CLuckyItem::GDLuckyItemSend(int aIndex) // OK
   429: {
   430: 	#if(GAMESERVER_UPDATE>=602)
   431: 
   432: 	if(gObjIsAccountValid(aIndex,gObj[aIndex].Account) == 0)
   433: 	{
   434: 		return;
   435: 	}
   436: 
   437: 	LPOBJ lpObj = &gObj[aIndex];
   438: 
   439: 	BYTE send[4096];
   440: 
   441: 	SDHP_LUCKY_ITEM_SEND pMsg;
   442: 
   443: 	pMsg.header.set(0x22,0x00,0);
   444: 
   445: 	int size = sizeof(pMsg);
   446: 
   447: 	pMsg.index = aIndex;
   448: 
   449: 	memcpy(pMsg.account,lpObj->Account,sizeof(pMsg.account));
   450: 
   451: 	pMsg.count = 0;
   452: 
   453: 	SDHP_LUCKY_ITEM2 info;
   454: 
   455: 	for(int n=0;n < INVENTORY_SIZE;n++)
   456: 	{
   457: 		if(lpObj->Inventory[n].IsItem() == 0)
   458: 		{
   459: 			continue;
   460: 		}
   461: 
   462: 		if(lpObj->Inventory[n].IsLuckyItem() == 0)
   463: 		{
   464: 			continue;
   465: 		}
   466: 
   467: 		info.slot = n;
   468: 
   469: 		info.serial = lpObj->Inventory[n].m_Serial;
   470: 
   471: 		memcpy(&send[size],&info,sizeof(info));
   472: 		size += sizeof(info);
   473: 
   474: 		pMsg.count++;
   475: 	}
   476: 
   477: 	if(pMsg.count > 0)
   478: 	{
   479: 		pMsg.header.size[0] = SET_NUMBERHB(size);
   480: 		pMsg.header.size[1] = SET_NUMBERLB(size);
   481: 
   482: 		memcpy(send,&pMsg,sizeof(pMsg));
   483: 
   484: 		gDataServerConnection.DataSend(send,size);
   485: 	}
   486: 
   487: 	#endif
   488: }
```

```
   534: 	{
   535: 		return;
   536: 	}

   546: 
   547: 	DataSend(aIndex, (UCHAR *)&pMsg, pMsg.h.size);
   548: }
```

```
    99: BYTE BoxExcOptions(int maxOptions)
   100: {
   101: 	BYTE NumberOfOptions = 0;
   102: 	BYTE RetOption = 0, TempOption = 0, deadlock = 15;
   103: 	BYTE ExcOrgArr[6];
   104: 
   105: 	if (maxOptions > 0)
   106: 	{
   107: 		if (maxOptions > 6)
   108: 			NumberOfOptions=6;
   109: 		else
   110: 			NumberOfOptions=rand()%maxOptions + 1;
   111: 
   112: 		//Exc Options IDs
   113: 		ExcOrgArr[0]=8;
   114: 		ExcOrgArr[1]=16;
   115: 		ExcOrgArr[2]=2;
   116: 		ExcOrgArr[3]=4;
   117: 		ExcOrgArr[4]=32;
   118: 		ExcOrgArr[5]=1;
   119: 
   120: 		if (NumberOfOptions >= 6)
   121: 		{
   122: 			RetOption = ExcOrgArr[0]+ExcOrgArr[1]+ExcOrgArr[2]+ExcOrgArr[3]+ExcOrgArr[4]+ExcOrgArr[5];
   123: 			return RetOption;
   124: 		}
   125: 
   126: 		if (NumberOfOptions == 5)
   127: 		{
   128: 			TempOption = ExcOrgArr[rand()%6];
   129: 
   130: 			RetOption = ExcOrgArr[0]+ExcOrgArr[1]+ExcOrgArr[2]+ExcOrgArr[3]+ExcOrgArr[4]+ExcOrgArr[5]-TempOption;
   131: 			return RetOption;
   132: 		}
   133: 
   134: 		if (NumberOfOptions == 4)
   135: 		{
   136: 			RetOption = ExcOrgArr[0]+ExcOrgArr[1]+ExcOrgArr[2]+ExcOrgArr[3]+ExcOrgArr[4]+ExcOrgArr[5];
   137: 			while(true)
   138: 			{
   139: 				TempOption = ExcOrgArr[rand()%6];
   140: 
   141: 				if ( (RetOption & TempOption) == TempOption )
   142: 				{
   143: 					RetOption -= TempOption;
   144: 					NumberOfOptions += 1;
   145: 				}
   146: 
   147: 				deadlock -= 1;
   148: 				if ( NumberOfOptions == 6 || deadlock == 0 )
   149: 					break;
   150: 			}
   151: 			return RetOption;
   152: 		}
   153: 
   154: 		if (NumberOfOptions == 3)
   155: 		{
   156: 			RetOption = 0;
   157: 			while(true)
   158: 			{
   159: 				TempOption = ExcOrgArr[rand()%6];
   160: 
   161: 				if ( (RetOption & TempOption) != TempOption )
   162: 				{
   163: 					RetOption += TempOption;
   164: 					NumberOfOptions -= 1;
   165: 				}
   166: 
   167: 				deadlock -= 1;
   168: 				if ( NumberOfOptions == 0 || deadlock == 0 )
   169: 					break;
   170: 			}
   171: 			return RetOption;
   172: 		}
   173: 
   174: 		if (NumberOfOptions == 2)
   175: 		{
   176: 			RetOption = 0;
   177: 			while(true)
   178: 			{
   179: 				TempOption = ExcOrgArr[rand()%6];
   180: 
   181: 				if ( (RetOption & TempOption) != TempOption )
   182: 				{
   183: 					RetOption += TempOption;
   184: 					NumberOfOptions -= 1;
   185: 				}
   186: 
   187: 				deadlock -= 1;

   190: 			}
   191: 			return RetOption;
   192: 		}
   193: 
   194: 		if (NumberOfOptions == 1)

   196: 			RetOption = ExcOrgArr[rand()%6];
   197: 			return RetOption;
   198: 		}
   199: 	}
   200: 	return RetOption;
```

```
    99: BYTE BoxExcOptions(int maxOptions)
   100: {
   101: 	BYTE NumberOfOptions = 0;
   102: 	BYTE RetOption = 0, TempOption = 0, deadlock = 15;
   103: 	BYTE ExcOrgArr[6];
   104: 
   105: 	if (maxOptions > 0)
   106: 	{
   107: 		if (maxOptions > 6)
   108: 			NumberOfOptions=6;
   109: 		else
   110: 			NumberOfOptions=rand()%maxOptions + 1;
   111: 
   112: 		//Exc Options IDs
   113: 		ExcOrgArr[0]=8;
   114: 		ExcOrgArr[1]=16;
   115: 		ExcOrgArr[2]=2;
   116: 		ExcOrgArr[3]=4;
   117: 		ExcOrgArr[4]=32;
   118: 		ExcOrgArr[5]=1;
   119: 
   120: 		if (NumberOfOptions >= 6)
   121: 		{
   122: 			RetOption = ExcOrgArr[0]+ExcOrgArr[1]+ExcOrgArr[2]+ExcOrgArr[3]+ExcOrgArr[4]+ExcOrgArr[5];
   123: 			return RetOption;
   124: 		}
   125: 
   126: 		if (NumberOfOptions == 5)
   127: 		{
   128: 			TempOption = ExcOrgArr[rand()%6];
   129: 
   130: 			RetOption = ExcOrgArr[0]+ExcOrgArr[1]+ExcOrgArr[2]+ExcOrgArr[3]+ExcOrgArr[4]+ExcOrgArr[5]-TempOption;
   131: 			return RetOption;
   132: 		}
   133: 
   134: 		if (NumberOfOptions == 4)
   135: 		{
   136: 			RetOption = ExcOrgArr[0]+ExcOrgArr[1]+ExcOrgArr[2]+ExcOrgArr[3]+ExcOrgArr[4]+ExcOrgArr[5];
   137: 			while(true)
   138: 			{
   139: 				TempOption = ExcOrgArr[rand()%6];
   140: 
   141: 				if ( (RetOption & TempOption) == TempOption )
   142: 				{
   143: 					RetOption -= TempOption;
   144: 					NumberOfOptions += 1;
   145: 				}
   146: 
   147: 				deadlock -= 1;
   148: 				if ( NumberOfOptions == 6 || deadlock == 0 )
   149: 					break;
   150: 			}
   151: 			return RetOption;
   152: 		}
   153: 
   154: 		if (NumberOfOptions == 3)
   155: 		{
   156: 			RetOption = 0;
   157: 			while(true)
   158: 			{
   159: 				TempOption = ExcOrgArr[rand()%6];
   160: 
   161: 				if ( (RetOption & TempOption) != TempOption )
   162: 				{
   163: 					RetOption += TempOption;
   164: 					NumberOfOptions -= 1;
   165: 				}
   166: 
   167: 				deadlock -= 1;
   168: 				if ( NumberOfOptions == 0 || deadlock == 0 )
   169: 					break;
   170: 			}
   171: 			return RetOption;
   172: 		}
   173: 
   174: 		if (NumberOfOptions == 2)
   175: 		{
   176: 			RetOption = 0;
   177: 			while(true)
   178: 			{
   179: 				TempOption = ExcOrgArr[rand()%6];
   180: 
   181: 				if ( (RetOption & TempOption) != TempOption )
   182: 				{
   183: 					RetOption += TempOption;
   184: 					NumberOfOptions -= 1;
   185: 				}
   186: 
   187: 				deadlock -= 1;
   188: 				if ( NumberOfOptions == 0 || deadlock == 0 )
   189: 					break;
   190: 			}
   191: 			return RetOption;
   192: 		}
   193: 
   194: 		if (NumberOfOptions == 1)
   195: 		{
   196: 			RetOption = ExcOrgArr[rand()%6];
   197: 			return RetOption;
   198: 		}
   199: 	}
   200: 	return RetOption;
   201: }
```

```
   242: void CSetItemOption::CalcSetItemOption(LPOBJ lpObj,bool flag) // OK
   243: {
   244: 	lpObj->IsFullSetItem = 0;
   245: 
   246: 	for(int n=0;n < MAX_SET_ITEM_OPTION;n++)
   247: 	{

   251: 		{
   252: 			continue;

   259: 			continue;
   260: 		}
   261: 
   262: 		for(int i=0;i < (ItemOptionCount-1);i++)
   263: 		{
   264: 			for(int o=0;o < MAX_SET_ITEM_OPTION_INDEX;o++)
   265: 			{
   266: 				if(lpInfo->OptionTable[i][o].Index != -1)

   268: 					this->InsertOption(lpObj,lpInfo->OptionTable[i][o].Index,lpInfo->OptionTable[i][o].Value,flag);
   269: 				}
   270: 			}
   271: 		}
   272: 
   273: 		if((ItemOptionCount-1) == this->GetSetItemMaxOptionCount(lpInfo->Index))

   275: 			for(int o=0;o < MAX_SET_ITEM_FULL_OPTION_TABLE;o++)
   276: 			{
   277: 				if(lpInfo->FullOptionTable[o].Index != -1)

   280: 				}
   281: 			}
   282: 
   283: 			lpObj->IsFullSetItem = 1;
   284: 		}
   285: 	}
   286: }
```

```
    55: 	{
    56: 		while(true)

    59: 			{
    60: 				break;
    61: 			}
    62: 
    63: 			if(strcmp("end",lpMemScript->GetString()) == 0)
    64: 			{

    68: 			SET_ITEM_OPTION_INFO info;

    75: 
    76: 			for(int n=0;n < MAX_SET_ITEM_OPTION_TABLE;n++)
    77: 			{
    78: 				for(int i=0;i < MAX_SET_ITEM_OPTION_INDEX;i++)
    79: 				{
    80: 					info.OptionTable[n][i].Index = lpMemScript->GetAsNumber();
    81: 					info.OptionTable[n][i].Value = lpMemScript->GetAsNumber();

    85: 			for(int o=0;o < MAX_SET_ITEM_FULL_OPTION_TABLE;o++)

   102: void CSetItemOption::SetInfo(SET_ITEM_OPTION_INFO info) // OK
   103: {
   104: 	if(info.Index < 0 || info.Index >= MAX_SET_ITEM_OPTION)
   105: 	{

   107: 	}

   109: 	this->m_SetItemOptionInfo[info.Index] = info;
   110: }
   111: 
   112: SET_ITEM_OPTION_INFO* CSetItemOption::GetInfo(int index) // OK
   113: {
   114: 	if(index < 0 || index >= MAX_SET_ITEM_OPTION)
   115: 	{
   116: 		return 0;

   120: 	{

   130: 	{

   132: 	}
   133: 
   134: 	return 1;
```
