```
   136: 
   137: int CSetItemOption::GetSetItemMaxOptionCount(int index) // OK
   138: {
   139: 	SET_ITEM_OPTION_INFO* lpInfo = this->GetInfo(index);
   140: 
   141: 	if(lpInfo == 0)
   142: 	{
   143: 		return 0;
   144: 	}
   145: 
   146: 	int count = 0;
   147: 
   148: 	for(int n=0;n < MAX_SET_ITEM_OPTION_TABLE;n++)
   149: 	{
   150: 		for(int i=0;i < MAX_SET_ITEM_OPTION_INDEX;i++)
   151: 		{
   152: 			if(lpInfo->OptionTable[n][i].Index != -1)
   153: 			{
   154: 				count++;
   155: 				break;
   156: 			}
   157: 		}
   158: 	}
   159: 
   160: 	return count;
   161: }
   162: 
   163: int CSetItemOption::GetInventorySetItemOptionCount(LPOBJ lpObj,int index) // OK
   164: {
   165: 	int OptionCount = 0;
   166: 	int WeaponOptionIndex = 0;
   167: 	int RingOptionIndex = 0;
   168: 
   169: 	for(int n=0;n < INVENTORY_WEAR_SIZE;n++)
   170: 	{
   171: 		if(lpObj->Inventory[n].IsItem() == 0 || lpObj->Inventory[n].IsSetItem() == 0 || lpObj->Inventory[n].m_IsValidItem == 0)
   172: 		{
   173: 			continue;
   174: 		}
   175: 
   176: 		if(gSetItemType.CheckSetItemType(lpObj->Inventory[n].m_Index) == 0)
   177: 		{
   178: 			continue;
   179: 		}
   180: 
   181: 		int OptionIndex = gSetItemType.GetSetItemOptionIndex(lpObj->Inventory[n].m_Index,((lpObj->Inventory[n].m_SetOption & 3)-1));
   182: 
   183: 		if(OptionIndex == 0 || OptionIndex != index)
   184: 		{
   185: 			continue;
   186: 		}
   187: 
   188: 		if(n == 0 || n == 1)
   189: 		{
   190: 			if(WeaponOptionIndex == 0)
   191: 			{
   192: 				WeaponOptionIndex = OptionIndex;
   193: 			}
   194: 			else if(WeaponOptionIndex == OptionIndex && lpObj->Inventory[0].m_Index == lpObj->Inventory[1].m_Index)
   195: 			{
   196: 				continue;
   197: 			}
   198: 		}
   199: 		else if(n == 10 || n == 11)
   200: 		{
   201: 			if(RingOptionIndex == 0)
   202: 			{
   203: 				RingOptionIndex = OptionIndex;
   204: 			}
   205: 			else if(RingOptionIndex == OptionIndex && lpObj->Inventory[10].m_Index == lpObj->Inventory[11].m_Index)
   206: 			{
   207: 				continue;
   208: 			}
   209: 		}
   210: 
   211: 		OptionCount++;
   212: 	}
   213: 
   214: 	return OptionCount;
   215: }
   216: 
   217: void CSetItemOption::CalcSetItemStat(LPOBJ lpObj) // OK
   218: {
   219: 	for(int n=0;n < INVENTORY_WEAR_SIZE;n++)
   220: 	{
   221: 		if(lpObj->Inventory[n].IsItem() == 0 || lpObj->Inventory[n].IsSetItem() == 0)
   222: 		{
   223: 			continue;
   224: 		}
   225: 
   226: 		if(gSetItemType.CheckSetItemType(lpObj->Inventory[n].m_Index) == 0)
   227: 		{
   228: 			continue;
   229: 		}
   230: 
   231: 		int ItemOption = gSetItemType.GetSetItemStatType(lpObj->Inventory[n].m_Index);
   232: 
   233: 		if(ItemOption == -1)
   234: 		{
   235: 			continue;
   236: 		}
   237: 
   238: 		this->InsertOption(lpObj,ItemOption,(((lpObj->Inventory[n].m_SetOption >> 2) & 3)*5),1);
   239: 	}
   240: }
   241: 
   242: void CSetItemOption::CalcSetItemOption(LPOBJ lpObj,bool flag) // OK
   243: {
   244: 	lpObj->IsFullSetItem = 0;
   245: 
   246: 	for(int n=0;n < MAX_SET_ITEM_OPTION;n++)
   247: 	{
   248: 		SET_ITEM_OPTION_INFO* lpInfo = this->GetInfo(n);
   249: 
   250: 		if(lpInfo == 0)
   251: 		{
   252: 			continue;
   253: 		}
   254: 
   255: 		int ItemOptionCount = this->GetInventorySetItemOptionCount(lpObj,lpInfo->Index);
   256: 
   257: 		if(ItemOptionCount == 0)
   258: 		{
   259: 			continue;
   260: 		}
   261: 
   262: 		for(int i=0;i < (ItemOptionCount-1);i++)
   263: 		{
   264: 			for(int o=0;o < MAX_SET_ITEM_OPTION_INDEX;o++)
   265: 			{
   266: 				if(lpInfo->OptionTable[i][o].Index != -1)
   267: 				{
   268: 					this->InsertOption(lpObj,lpInfo->OptionTable[i][o].Index,lpInfo->OptionTable[i][o].Value,flag);
   269: 				}
   270: 			}
   271: 		}
   272: 
   273: 		if((ItemOptionCount-1) == this->GetSetItemMaxOptionCount(lpInfo->Index))
   274: 		{
   275: 			for(int o=0;o < MAX_SET_ITEM_FULL_OPTION_TABLE;o++)
   276: 			{
   277: 				if(lpInfo->FullOptionTable[o].Index != -1)
   278: 				{
   279: 					this->InsertOption(lpObj,lpInfo->FullOptionTable[o].Index,lpInfo->FullOptionTable[o].Value,flag);
   280: 				}
   281: 			}
   282: 
   283: 			lpObj->IsFullSetItem = 1;
   284: 		}
   285: 	}
   286: }

   289: {
```

```
    84: void CShopManager::LoadShop() // OK
    85: {
    86: 	std::map<int,int> LoadShop;
    87: 
    88: 	char wildcard_path[MAX_PATH];
    89: 
    90: 	wsprintf(wildcard_path,"%s*",gPath.GetFullPath("Shop\\"));
    91: 
    92: 	WIN32_FIND_DATA data;
    93: 
    94: 	HANDLE file = FindFirstFile(wildcard_path,&data);
    95: 
    96: 	if(file == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE)
    97: 	{
    98: 		return;
    99: 	}
   100: 
   101: 	do
   102: 	{
   103: 		if((data.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY) == 0)
   104: 		{
   105: 			if(isdigit(data.cFileName[0]) != 0 && isdigit(data.cFileName[1]) != 0 && isdigit(data.cFileName[2]) != 0)
   106: 			{
   107: 				if(data.cFileName[3] == ' ' && data.cFileName[4] == '-' && data.cFileName[5] == ' ')
   108: 				{
   109: 					std::map<int,SHOP_MANAGER_INFO>::iterator it = this->m_ShopManagerInfo.find(atoi(data.cFileName));
   110: 
   111: 					if(it != this->m_ShopManagerInfo.end())

   115: 							char path[MAX_PATH];
   116: 
   117: 							wsprintf(path,"Shop\\%s",data.cFileName);
   118: 
   119: 							it->second.Shop.Load(gPath.GetFullPath(path));
   120: 
   121: 							LoadShop.insert(std::pair<int,int>(it->first,1));
   122: 						}
   123: 					}
   124: 				}
   125: 			}
   126: 		}
   127: 	}
   128: 	while(FindNextFile(file,&data) != 0);
   129: }
```

```
   504: void CSocketItemOption::CalcSocketItemOption(LPOBJ lpObj,bool flag) // OK
   505: {
   506: 	int ItemOptionTypeCount[MAX_SOCKET_ITEM_OPTION_TYPE] = {0,0,0,0,0,0};
   507: 
   508: 	for(int n=0;n < INVENTORY_WEAR_SIZE;n++)
   509: 	{
   510: 		if(lpObj->Inventory[n].IsItem() == 0 || lpObj->Inventory[n].IsSocketItem() == 0)
   511: 		{
   512: 			continue;
   513: 		}
   514: 
   515: 		for(int i=0;i < MAX_SOCKET_ITEM_OPTION_TABLE;i++)
   516: 		{
   517: 			if(lpObj->Inventory[n].m_SocketOption[i] != SOCKET_ITEM_OPTION_NONE && lpObj->Inventory[n].m_SocketOption[i] != SOCKET_ITEM_OPTION_EMPTY)
   518: 			{
   519: 				int ItemOption = lpObj->Inventory[n].m_SocketOption[i]%MAX_SOCKET_ITEM_OPTION;
   520: 
   521: 				SOCKET_ITEM_OPTION_INFO* lpInfo = this->GetInfo(ItemOption);
   522: 
   523: 				if(lpInfo == 0)
   524: 				{
   525: 					continue;
   526: 				}
   527: 
   528: 				int ItemOptionLevel = lpObj->Inventory[n].m_SocketOption[i]/MAX_SOCKET_ITEM_OPTION;
   529: 
   530: 				if(ItemOptionLevel < 0 || ItemOptionLevel >= MAX_SOCKET_ITEM_OPTION_TABLE)
   531: 				{
   532: 					continue;
   533: 				}
   534: 
   535: 				this->InsertOption(lpObj,lpInfo->Index,lpInfo->ValueTable[ItemOptionLevel],flag);
   536: 
   537: 				ItemOptionTypeCount[(lpInfo->Type-1)]++;
   538: 			}
   539: 		}
   540: 
   541: 		if(lpObj->Inventory[n].m_SocketOptionBonus != SOCKET_ITEM_BONUS_OPTION_NONE)
   542: 		{
   543: 			SOCKET_ITEM_BONUS_OPTION_INFO* lpBonusInfo = this->GetBonusInfo(lpObj->Inventory[n].m_SocketOptionBonus);
   544: 
   545: 			if(lpBonusInfo == 0)
   546: 			{
   547: 				continue;

   553: 
   554: 	this->CalcSocketItemPackageOption(lpObj,ItemOptionTypeCount,flag);

   557: void CSocketItemOption::CalcSocketItemPackageOption(LPOBJ lpObj,int* ItemOptionTypeCount,bool flag) // OK
```

```
   124: void CTrade::CGTradeRequestRecv(PMSG_TRADE_REQUEST_RECV* lpMsg,int aIndex) // OK
   125: {
   126: 	if(gServerInfo.m_TradeSwitch == 0)
   127: 	{
   128: 		return;
   129: 	}
   130: 
   131: 	LPOBJ lpObj = &gObj[aIndex];
   132: 
   133: 	if(gObjIsConnectedGP(aIndex) == 0)
   134: 	{
   135: 		return;
   136: 	}
   137: 
   138: 	int bIndex = MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->index[0],lpMsg->index[1]);
   139: 
   140: 	if(gObjIsConnectedGP(bIndex) == 0)
   141: 	{
   142: 		return;
   143: 	}
   144: 
   145: 	LPOBJ lpTarget = &gObj[bIndex];
   146: 
   147: 	if(lpObj->Interface.use != 0 || lpTarget->Interface.use != 0)
   148: 	{
   149: 		return;
   150: 	}
   151: 
   152: 	if(lpObj->TradeDuel != 0)
   153: 	{
   154: 		return;
   155: 	}
   156: 
   157: 	if(lpTarget->TradeDuel != 0)
   158: 	{
   159: 		return;
   160: 	}
   161: 
   162: 	if(CA_MAP_RANGE(lpTarget->Map) != 0 || DS_MAP_RANGE(lpTarget->Map) != 0 || BC_MAP_RANGE(lpTarget->Map) != 0 || CC_MAP_RANGE(lpTarget->Map) != 0 || IT_MAP_RANGE(lpTarget->Map) != 0 || DA_MAP_RANGE(lpTarget->Map) != 0 || DG_MAP_RANGE(lpTarget->Map) != 0 || IG_MAP_RANGE(lpTarget->Map) != 0)
   163: 	{
   164: 		this->GCTradeResponseSend(aIndex,0,lpTarget->Name,0,0);
   165: 		return;
   166: 	}
   167: 
   168: 	if(lpObj->X < (lpTarget->X-2) || lpObj->X > (lpTarget->X+2) || lpObj->Y < (lpTarget->Y-2) || lpObj->Y > (lpTarget->Y+2))
   169: 	{
   170: 		this->GCTradeResponseSend(aIndex,0,lpTarget->Name,0,0);
   171: 		return;
   172: 	}
   173: 
   174: 	if(lpObj->PShopOpen != 0 || lpTarget->PShopOpen != 0)
   175: 	{
   176: 		this->GCTradeResponseSend(aIndex,0,lpTarget->Name,0,0);
   177: 		return;
   178: 	}
   179: 
   180: 	if(gServerInfo.m_PKDisableTrade == 1 && lpObj->PKLevel >= 5)
   181: 	{
   182: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpObj->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(861));
   183: 		return;
   184: 	}
   185: 
   186: 	if(gServerInfo.m_PKDisableTrade == 1 && lpTarget->PKLevel >= 5)
   187: 	{
   188: 		gNotice.GCNoticeSend(lpObj->Index,1,0,0,0,0,0,gMessage.GetMessage(862));
   189: 		return;
   190: 	}
   191: 
   192: 	if(gEventHideAndSeek.EventHideAndSeekTrade(aIndex,bIndex) == 1)
   193: 	{
   194: 		return;
   195: 	}
   196: 
   197: 	if(gEventPvP.EventPvPTradeJoin(aIndex,bIndex) == 1)
   198: 	{
   199: 		return;
   200: 	}
   201: 
   202: 	if(gEventRussianRoulette.EventRussianRouletteTrade(aIndex,bIndex) == 1)
   203: 	{
   204: 		return;
   205: 	}
   206: 
   207: 	if((lpTarget->Option & 1) == 0)
   208: 	{
   209: 		this->GCTradeResponseSend(aIndex,0,lpTarget->Name,0,0);
   210: 		return;
   211: 	}
   212: 
   213: 	//MC bot
   214: 	// SCF BOTS
   215: 
   216: 	int	number = MAKE_NUMBERW(lpMsg->index[0],lpMsg->index[1]);
   217: 
   218: 	if ( OBJMAX_RANGE(number) == FALSE )
   219: 	{
   220: 		LogAdd(LOG_BLACK,"Error : %s %d (%d)", __FILE__, __LINE__, number);
   221: 		return;
   222: 	}
   223: 	if ( number == aIndex )
   224: 	{
   225: 		return;
   226: 	}
   227: 
   228: 	if(ObjBotBuff.TradeOpen(aIndex,number) == 1)
   229: 	{
   230: 		return;
   231: 	}
   232: 
   233: 	lpObj->Interface.use = 1;
   234: 	lpObj->Interface.type = INTERFACE_TRADE;
   235: 	lpObj->Interface.state = 0;
   236: 	lpObj->InterfaceTime = GetTickCount();
   237: 	lpObj->TargetNumber = bIndex;
   238: 
   239: 	lpTarget->Interface.use = 1;
   240: 	lpTarget->Interface.type = INTERFACE_TRADE;
   241: 	lpTarget->Interface.state = 0;
   242: 	lpTarget->InterfaceTime = GetTickCount();
   243: 	lpTarget->TargetNumber = aIndex;
   244: 
   245: 	this->GCTradeRequestSend(bIndex,lpObj->Name);
   246: }
```

```
   588: 	LPOBJ lpObj = &gObj[aIndex];
   589: 
   590: 	if(gObjIsConnectedGP(aIndex) == 0)
   591: 	{
   592: 		return;
   593: 	}
   594: 
   595: 	int bIndex = lpObj->TargetNumber;
   596: 
   597: 	if(gObjIsConnectedGP(bIndex) == 0)
   598: 	{
   599: 		return;
   600: 	}
   601: 
   602: 	LPOBJ lpTarget = &gObj[bIndex];
   603: 
   604: 	if(lpObj->Interface.use == 0 || lpObj->Interface.type != INTERFACE_TRADE || lpObj->Interface.state == 0)
   605: 	{
   606: 		return;
   607: 	}
   608: 
   609: 	if(lpTarget->Interface.use == 0 || lpTarget->Interface.type != INTERFACE_TRADE || lpTarget->Interface.state == 0)
   610: 	{
   611: 		return;
   612: 	}
   613: 
   614: 	this->ResetTrade(aIndex);
   615: 	this->GCTradeResultSend(aIndex,0);
   616: 
   617: 	this->ResetTrade(bIndex);
   618: 	this->GCTradeResultSend(bIndex,0);
   619: }
```

```
   706: void CTrade::TradeDuel(LPOBJ lpObj, LPOBJ lpTarget) // OK
   707: {
   708: 	if(gObjInventoryTransaction(lpObj->Index) == 0 || gObjInventoryTransaction(lpTarget->Index) == 0)
   709: 	{
   710: 		gObjInventoryCommit(lpObj->Index);
   711: 
   712: 		lpObj->Interface.use = 0;
   713: 		lpObj->Interface.type = INTERFACE_NONE;
   714: 		lpObj->Interface.state = 0;
   715: 		lpObj->TargetNumber = -1;
   716: 		lpObj->TradeDuel = 0;
   717: 
   718: 		this->GCTradeResponseSend(lpObj->Index,0,lpTarget->Name,0,0);
   719: 
   720: 		gObjInventoryCommit(lpTarget->Index);
   721: 
   722: 		lpTarget->Interface.use = 0;
   723: 		lpTarget->Interface.type = INTERFACE_NONE;
   724: 		lpTarget->Interface.state = 0;
   725: 		lpTarget->TargetNumber = -1;
   726: 		lpTarget->TradeDuel = 0;
   727: 
   728: 		this->GCTradeResponseSend(lpTarget->Index,0,lpObj->Name,0,0);
   729: 		return;
   730: 	}
   731: 
   732: 	this->ClearTrade(lpObj);
   733: 
   734: 	lpObj->Interface.use = 1;
   735: 	lpObj->Interface.type = INTERFACE_TRADE;
   736: 	lpObj->Interface.state = 1;
   737: 	lpObj->TradeOk = 0;
   738: 	lpObj->TradeOkTime = 0;
   739: 	lpObj->TradeMoney = 0;
   740: 	lpObj->InterfaceTime = GetTickCount();
   741: 	lpObj->TargetNumber = lpTarget->Index;
   742: 	lpObj->TradeDuel = 1;
   743: 
   744: 	this->GCTradeResponseSend(lpObj->Index,1,lpTarget->Name,lpTarget->Level,lpTarget->GuildNumber);
   745: 
   746: 	this->ClearTrade(lpTarget);
   747: 
   748: 	lpTarget->Interface.use = 1;
   749: 	lpTarget->Interface.type = INTERFACE_TRADE;
   750: 	lpTarget->Interface.state = 1;
   751: 	lpTarget->TradeOk = 0;
   752: 	lpTarget->TradeOkTime = 0;
   753: 	lpTarget->TradeMoney = 0;
   754: 	lpTarget->InterfaceTime = GetTickCount();
   755: 	lpTarget->TargetNumber = lpObj->Index;
   756: 	lpTarget->TradeDuel = 1;
   757: 
   758: 	this->GCTradeResponseSend(lpTarget->Index,1,lpObj->Name,lpObj->Level,lpObj->GuildNumber);
   759: 
   760: }
   761: 
   762: void CTrade::TradeDuelEnd(LPOBJ lpObj, LPOBJ lpTarget)
   763: {
   764: 
   765: 	#if(GAMESERVER_UPDATE>=701)
   766: 
   767: 	if(gPentagramSystem.CheckExchangePentagramItem(lpObj) == 0 || gPentagramSystem.CheckExchangePentagramItem(lpTarget) == 0)
   768: 	{
   769: 		this->ResetTrade(lpObj->Index);
   770: 		this->ResetTrade(lpTarget->Index);
   771: 		return;
   772: 	}
   773: 
   774: 	#endif
   775: 
   776: 	if(gObjCheckMaxMoney(lpObj->Index,lpTarget->TradeMoney) != 0)
   777: 	{
   778: 		lpObj->Money += lpTarget->TradeMoney;
   779: 		GCMoneySend(lpObj->Index,lpObj->Money);
   780: 	}
   781: 
   782: 	for(int n=0;n < TRADE_SIZE;n++)
   783: 	{
   784: 		if(lpObj->Trade[n].IsItem() != 0)
   785: 		{
   786: 			if((lpObj->Trade[n].m_Slot=gItemManager.InventoryInsertItem(lpObj->Index,lpObj->Trade[n])) == 0xFF)
   787: 			{
   788: 				//return 0;
   789: 			}
   790: 		}
   791: 	}
   792: 
   793: 	for(int n=0;n < TRADE_SIZE;n++)
   794: 	{
   795: 		if(lpTarget->Trade[n].IsItem() != 0)
   796: 		{
   797: 			if((lpTarget->Trade[n].m_Slot=gItemManager.InventoryInsertItem(lpObj->Index,lpTarget->Trade[n])) == 0xFF)
   798: 			{
   799: 				//return 0;
   800: 			}
   801: 		}
   802: 	}
   803: 
   804: 
   805: 
   806: 	#if(GAMESERVER_UPDATE>=701)
   807: 
   808: 	gPentagramSystem.ExchangePentagramItem(lpObj,lpTarget);
   809: 
   810: 	gPentagramSystem.ExchangePentagramItem(lpTarget,lpObj);
   811: 
   812: 	#endif
   813: 
   814: 	gObjInventoryCommit(lpObj->Index);
   815: 
   816: 	gItemManager.GCItemListSend(lpObj->Index);
   817: 
   818: 	#if(GAMESERVER_UPDATE>=802)
   819: 
   820: 	gEventInventory.GCEventItemListSend(lpObj->Index);
   821: 
   822: 	#endif
   823: 
   824: 	//gObjectManager.CharacterMakePreviewCharSet(lpObj->Index);
   825: 
   826: 	GDCharacterInfoSaveSend(lpObj->Index);
   827: 
   828: 	this->ClearTrade(lpObj);
   829: 
   830: 	gObjInventoryCommit(lpTarget->Index);
   831: 
   832: 	gItemManager.GCItemListSend(lpTarget->Index);
   833: 
   834: 	#if(GAMESERVER_UPDATE>=802)
   835: 
   836: 	gEventInventory.GCEventItemListSend(lpTarget->Index);
   837: 
   838: 	#endif
   839: 
   840: 	//gObjectManager.CharacterMakePreviewCharSet(lpTarget->Index);
   841: 
   842: 	GCMoneySend(lpTarget->Index,lpTarget->Money);
   843: 
   844: 	GDCharacterInfoSaveSend(lpTarget->Index);
   845: 
   846: 	this->ClearTrade(lpTarget);
   847: 
   848: 	lpObj->Interface.use = 0;
   849: 	lpObj->Interface.type = INTERFACE_NONE;
   850: 	lpObj->Interface.state = 0;
   851: 	lpObj->TargetNumber = -1;
   852: 	lpObj->TradeOk = 0;
   853: 	lpObj->TradeOkTime = 0;
   854: 	lpObj->TradeMoney = 0;
   855: 	lpTarget->TradeDuel = 0;
   856: 
   857: 	lpTarget->Interface.use = 0;
   858: 	lpTarget->Interface.type = INTERFACE_NONE;
   859: 	lpTarget->Interface.state = 0;
   860: 	lpTarget->TargetNumber = -1;
   861: 	lpTarget->TradeOk = 0;
   862: 	lpTarget->TradeOkTime = 0;
   863: 	lpTarget->TradeMoney = 0;
   864: 	lpTarget->TradeDuel = 0;
   865: }
   866: 
   867: void CTrade::ResetDuelTrade(int aIndex) // OK
   868: {
   869: 	LPOBJ lpObj = &gObj[aIndex];
   870: 
   871: 	gObjInventoryRollback(aIndex);
   872: 
   873: 	gItemManager.GCItemListSend(aIndex);
   874: 
   875: 	#if(GAMESERVER_UPDATE>=802)
   876: 
   877: 	gEventInventory.GCEventItemListSend(aIndex);
   878: 
   879: 	#endif
   880: 
   881: 	GCMoneySend(aIndex,lpObj->Money);
   882: 
   883: 	gObjectManager.CharacterMakePreviewCharSet(aIndex);
   884: 
   885: 	gItemManager.GCItemEquipmentSend(aIndex);
   886: 
   887: 	this->ClearTrade(lpObj);
   888: 
   889: 	lpObj->Interface.use = 0;
   890: 	lpObj->Interface.type = INTERFACE_NONE;
   891: 	lpObj->Interface.state = 0;
   892: 	lpObj->TargetNumber = -1;
   893: 	lpObj->TradeOk = 0;
   894: 	lpObj->TradeOkTime = 0;
   895: 	lpObj->TradeMoney = 0;
   896: 	lpObj->TradeDuel = 0;
   897: }
```
